### 3. Android catalog audit (read-only, 2026-11-24) — PENDING STAFF VERIFICATION
- **What:** PROD catalog vs the Owner's latest Android supplier WhatsApp list: missing phones/variants, missing/additional colours, price differences, low-resolution images, unwanted white/coloured backgrounds, duplicates. Read-only SQL (`supabase db query --read-only`) + public Cloudinary 32 px samples. **No data was changed.**
- **Result:** **428 findings** (306 Confirmed / 122 Uncertain) across 124 products — Missing phone 46 (28 not on the website at all, 11 exist only as DRAFT), Missing variant 4, Missing colour 32, Additional colour 44, Additional variant 6, Price difference 77 (6 flagged as probable supplier typos: Flip 5 Rs 2,280,000; Camon 50 Pro Rs 1,040,000; C100x 6/128 Rs 615,000; Note 60x Rs 329,000; A6k 6/128 Rs 6,750; Xiaomi 17T Rs 22,500), Low-resolution image 102, Unwanted background 117, Duplicate listing 0. Colours compared literally (no aliases); lifestyle photos not flagged; "blurry" covered only by resolution.
- **Files:** staff HTML **`C:\Users\User\Desktop\PROD-Android-Catalog-Audit-2026-11-24.html`** (standalone, offline, mobile-friendly; shared via WhatsApp). Copies: `audit-reports/PROD-Android-Catalog-Audit-2026-11-24.html` and `.xlsx` in the repo folder (**untracked on purpose — never commit**). The HTML has per-finding status **Issue Confirmed / No Issue / OK / Pending** (saved in each browser's localStorage, per device), status filter, progress bar, and **Export Excel** (.xlsx built in the page) / **Export PDF** (print → Save as PDF) with the three statuses.
- **Pending:** staff review of all 428 findings, then Owner decisions (publish drafts, add missing phones/colours, price updates, image replacements). Each correction is a separate Owner-approved task; never act on the audit automatically. The audit was a snapshot of 2026-11-24 PROD data.

### 6. How to deploy safely (unchanged method — follow exactly)
1. Copy the PROD-live export (`E:\iso-export-listformatter-1125-src`) to a new `E:\iso-export-<name>-<mmdd>-src` (robocopy, exclude `node_modules dist .wrangler .firebase *.tsbuildinfo .env.local`; recreate the `node_modules` junction), add ONLY the approved files, diff it against the baseline.
2. DEV: copy `.env.local` in → `npm run build:dev` → delete `.env.local` → guard `GCLOUD_PROJECT=<project> node scripts/check-hosting-environment.mjs` (must pass for DEV, block PROD) → `npx firebase deploy --only hosting --project isolutions-development-cb1ea --non-interactive` → verify live assets byte-identical + routes 200.
3. PROD: `npx wrangler@4.150.0 deployments status` (record rollback) → `npm run build:prod` → guard (must pass PROD, block DEV) → compare chunks vs the live export's `dist` (only expected chunks change) → `npx wrangler@4.150.0 deploy --message "..."` → verify `isolutionspakistan.com` assets byte-identical, routes 200, `www` 301.
4. Then a focused Git sync commit (temporary index for mixed files), push, and update these four handover docs.

### 7. Rules that still apply
Owner = Muhammad Junaid (final decision-maker). Every DB write, migration, deploy, commit and push needs explicit Owner approval in chat. Never expose service-role keys or secrets. DEV first, then PROD. Read-only PROD SQL uses `npx supabase@3.4.0 db query --linked --read-only -f <file>` (the CLI link points at PROD). Do not transfer DEV test data to PROD. Do not commit HOLD work or audit artifacts.

## 1. Roles and mandatory working rules

| Role | Who | Does |
|---|---|---|
| **Owner** | Muhammad Junaid | Final authority. Approves every phase, DB write, migration, commit, push, deploy and PROD action. |
| **Developer** | Claude Code (sole developer) | Writes code, migrations and tests; runs read-only checks; executes only what the Owner approves in that message. |
| **Analyzer / QA** | ChatGPT (GPT Analyzer) | Reviews plans and Claude reports, does QA, writes Owner-approved prompts for Claude. Does not write production code. |

`AGENTS.md` still names Codex as implementation engineer; in practice Claude Code is the developer.

**Rules for every step**
- Short Roman Urdu with the Owner; **one step at a time.**
- **No coding without Owner approval.** Order: **localhost → Owner approval → DEV → PROD.** Never skip.
- **Every DB write, migration, upload, commit, push and deployment needs explicit Owner approval in that message.** The Owner may approve several named actions in one message (e.g. "A–E"); approval never carries over to later actions.
- Before a migration: confirm the target project ref, run a dry-run and **stop** if anything other than the approved file is listed. Take a read-only before/after snapshot (counts + row fingerprints of products, variants, stock ledger, media).
- Read-only checks run inside `begin transaction read only; … rollback;`.
- **Never guess product facts** (PTA, condition, SIM, colour, specs, images, battery health, warranty). Blank stays blank (NULL). No similar-model matching.
- Preserve uncommitted changes: no cleanup, reset, stash or discard. Never delete catalog data or test products.
- Preserve the approved storefront design and business rules; no redesign.
- **Don't repeat completed work, audits or tests** (§6).
- **Deploy only committed code** from a clean export (`git archive <sha>` + only the needed env file). Never deploy the working-tree `dist/`.
- Never request, print or store secrets. The Owner signs in to Admin; Claude never signs in for them and never clicks Update Website / Save / Publish / Create.
- If Claude Code's safety check or a tool policy blocks a command: stop and report; never work around it.
- **Project information is not saved to long-term memory**; these handover files are the record.
- Windows/Git Bash quirk: heredocs mangle `\\`; write files with the Write/Edit tools. `AdminApp.tsx` has CRLF line endings.

---

## 2. Architecture (locked)

- **Frontend:** React + Vite + TypeScript; one repo serves the storefront and `/admin` (Admin Studio). Admin code is a separate lazy chunk (`AdminApp-*.js`).
- **Hosting:** Firebase Hosting. DEV `isolutions-development-cb1ea.web.app`, PROD `isolutions-pakistan-production.web.app`. `.firebaserc` aliases `development`, `production`. No Cloudflare/OpenNext, no Vercel.
  - **2026-11-10/11 Owner exception:** after the Firebase PROD 509, the Owner approved testing **Cloudflare Workers Static Assets** (static files only, no OpenNext, no Worker script) and planning the production migration (§4I). `AGENTS.md` and the other governance files are **unchanged** — a permanent rule change needs a separate Owner decision.
  - **2026-11-12 (OWNER-REPORTED):** the production domain `isolutionspakistan.com` (root + `www`) is now served by the Cloudflare Worker `isolutions-pakistan-test` (Workers Static Assets, assets-only) — migration CLOSED (§4I). Firebase PROD (`web.app`) and DEV Hosting are unchanged. `AGENTS.md` and the governance files are still **unchanged**; the formal rule update remains a separate Owner decision.
- **Backend:** Supabase (PostgreSQL + Auth + RLS + Edge Functions). DEV `acwatgxkcyrdxpcljxjb`, PROD `nlxfwppywxumymbjkvwt`.
- **Media:** Cloudinary signed upload via Edge Function; only metadata in `product_media`. Delivery URLs via `cloudinaryDeliveryUrl` (`src/lib/cloudinary.ts`, optional `e_trim`).
- **Builds:** `npm run build:dev` (reads `.env.local`) / `npm run build:prod` (reads `.env.production.local`). `npm run dev` always connects to DEV. The Firebase predeploy guard (`scripts/check-hosting-environment.mjs`) refuses a bundle wired to the other environment. `npm run preview:dev|preview:prod` (`scripts/preview-dist.mjs`) serves a built `dist/` behind the same guard.
- **Storefront data:** customer pages read only through security-definer RPCs (`search_public_catalog`, `homepage_featured_products`, `frequently_bought_together`, …) that return published, real, active data only; RLS on catalog tables exposes only published real products.
- **Money** integer paisa · **Stock** = sum of `inventory_movements` · **SKUs** server-assigned per category prefix: `MB` mobile-phones, `AC` mobile-accessories, `GD` gadgets, `IP` tablets, `MC` laptops. DEV and PROD SKUs are independent.
- **Categories:** `gadgets` (top level) with children `tablets` and `laptops`; `mobile-phones`; `mobile-accessories`. Slugs are unchanged and must not be renamed.

### Locked Owner decisions
- **Checkout:** server-side `create_storefront_order`; Cash on Delivery or Bank Transfer only.
- **Shipping:** below Rs 10,000 → Standard Rs 200, Fast Rs 400; Rs 10,000+ → Standard free, Fast Rs 200 (constants in `src/lib/orders.ts`, mirrored by the server).
- **Delivery:** mobile phones Karachi only; **iPads, tablets and MacBooks Karachi only**; gadgets/accessories nationwide; any cart with a Karachi-only item becomes Karachi-only.
- **Android phones:** PTA Approved, Brand New, 1 Year warranty, Karachi only. Android Stock Update: latest list is the truth; listed variants → list price, visible, stock 30; missing variants of published phones hidden (never deleted); one all-or-nothing call per phone.
- **iPhone Stock Update:** §5.1. **Warranty:** §5.4. **Gadgets:** §5A. **Tablets & MacBooks:** §5B. **Images:** §7. **Specifications:** §8. **PDP:** §9. **Bulk Import:** §10.
- **Windows laptops are not sold.** Only Apple MacBooks in `laptops`; a non-Apple laptop cannot be published (§5B).
- **SIM values (database):** `physical_sim`, `esim`, `physical_plus_esim`, `dual_esim`, `dual_physical_sim` (two physical SIMs). Blank = NULL, never inferred. SIM is not part of variant identity.
- **Official WhatsApp Business number (Owner-confirmed):** `923132344912` (0313 2344912), stored in `src/lib/storefrontContact.ts`.
- **"100% Authentic — Official Product"** PDP card: wording stays unchanged on all products (Owner decision 2026-10-24).
- **PDP image browsing never changes storage, variant or price** (Owner decision 2026-10-25, §9.2).
- **Colours:** Gray stored as Grey. ZTE = "ZTE Focus Pro 5G". A57 blue differs by environment (§13 #1).
- **Not authorized:** customer accounts, card/installment gateways, promotions engine, production commerce at scale, internal live chat.
- **Catalog operations are the Owner's (Owner decision 2026-10-31):** routine product uploads, stock updates, specifications, images and publishing are done by the Owner through the existing Admin Studio workflows (Stock Update pages, Media, Spec Upload, Bulk Publish). They are **not** pending development tasks; those modules stay CLOSED. **Android Mobiles is the first category the Owner selected for product uploading** (Owner decision 2026-11-01). Product information is never guessed; missing information stays NULL.

**Committing from mixed files:** write only the approved file versions into a **temporary index** (`GIT_INDEX_FILE=<tmp> git read-tree HEAD`, `git add -- <files>`, for a partial file `git hash-object -w --path=<file> <prepared copy>` + `git update-index --cacheinfo`), `git commit`, then put the same blobs into the real index so nothing shows as staged. Used for every commit since `9176c8d`.

**Line endings:** no `.gitattributes`, `core.autocrlf=true`. In a fresh Windows `git archive` export two older tests fail only because of CRLF (`androidStockPhoneRpc.test.mjs` and the `homepage_featured_products` check in `phase4.test.mjs`) — not regressions. **Known baseline in a clean export (2026-10-30):** exactly 3 failures — these two plus the stale `securityRls` RPC list — identical on `25f0914` and `704dc83` (clean `704dc83` export: 1036 pass, 3 fail). Outside current scope; don't re-investigate.

**Cutover plan (not executed; each step needs explicit Owner approval):**
1. In the Pending zone: set root A and `www` CNAME to **DNS only**; enable **Always Use HTTPS** (no public effect while Pending).
2. At GoDaddy: replace the nameservers (now Afternic) with the 2 Cloudflare nameservers shown in the zone. Keep the backups.
3. Wait until the zone is **Active** (Cloudflare docs: up to 24 h; email on activation). **Worker Custom Domains and Routes need an active zone** (verified) — they cannot be attached before.
4. Worker → Domains & Routes: add custom domain `isolutionspakistan.com` (replaces the Firebase A record). Then delete the `www` CNAME (a Custom Domain cannot be created over an existing CNAME) and add `www.isolutionspakistan.com`.
5. Confirm both certificates are Active, then a Single Redirect `www` → `https://isolutionspakistan.com` (301, keep path + query).
6. Live checks: root 200 + entry `index-BF-3Bly0.js`; `www` 301; `portal` and `iphone18` 200 from Vercel; PROD Supabase only; Owner Admin login; catalog / cart / checkout screen without an order.
- **STOP** if a Cloudflare record differs from the backup, a payment/plan prompt appears, a certificate is not Active, `portal`/`iphone18` fail, or the Worker version is not `4e1b0eff…`.
- Rollback: DNS problem → fix the record from the backup, or point the nameservers back (note: the old GoDaddy `ns57`/`ns58` zone and Afternic parking both leave the root without a working site). Worker problem → roll back to an earlier Worker version or redeploy `E:\iso-export-d712c58-cf\dist`.
- Later (separate approval): security headers via a committed `public\_headers` (HSTS `max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, `X-Frame-Options: DENY`; Permissions-Policy without `web-share`/`clipboard`; CSP only as Report-Only first — the bundle contains a direct `eval`); optional production Worker name; whether to commit the Worker config; formal `AGENTS.md` rule update.

- **VERIFIED:** the storefront router reads `location.pathname` once per load (`src/StorefrontApp.tsx` `StorefrontApp`); navigation uses `<a href>` / `window.location.assign` = **full-page reloads**. Only exception: `history.replaceState` when Shop filters change. `/admin*` is a separate lazy-loaded `AdminApp` (`src/App.tsx`).
- **Recommended architecture (not implemented):** one env-gated GTM loader (e.g. a PROD-only build variable), **skipped on `/admin*`**; one GA4 Google tag inside GTM (no separate gtag.js in code); normal `page_view` per load (no History Change trigger); later e-commerce events only via sanitized `dataLayer` pushes from code — never DOM scraping or form-field variables. DEV either no GTM or a GTM Environment; DEV must never send hits to the PROD GA4 stream.
- **Enhanced Measurement guidance:** turn off "page changes based on browser history events" (site reloads per page; Shop `replaceState` may add extra page views — ASSUMED, verify in DebugView) and "form interactions" (would fire on checkout even when validation fails); keep site search (`q`) **or** a custom `search` event, not both; WhatsApp links will appear as automatic outbound `click`.

(High) **customer PII** — checkout collects name, phone, email, address, notes (sent only to the RPC, not in URLs — VERIFIED); never push them to `dataLayer`. (Medium) **search text in URL** `/shop?q=` reaches GA4 `page_location` (strip/redact `q` or accept by policy). (Medium) DEV/PROD drift. (Medium) PROD deploy path is now the Cloudflare Worker clean-export route; `AGENTS.md` still says not to introduce Cloudflare (formal rule update pending). (Low) `document.title` never changes per page. (Low) Privacy Policy does not mention analytics/cookies.

### 4L. Order Management Enhancement (2026-11-13 — planning only, nothing implemented)

**Existing order system (VERIFIED read-only, 2026-11-13):**
- Browser: `placeOrder` (`src/StorefrontApp.tsx`) → `createStorefrontOrder` (`src/lib/orders.ts`) → RPC **`create_storefront_order`**; current definition in `supabase/migrations/202610050001_checkout_coupons_v1.sql` (older overload dropped there). One transaction: input validation → rate limits (5/phone, 20/IP per 15 min) → variants locked, price + stock re-read server-side → delivery eligibility, server shipping fee, locked coupon → `order_number` = `ISP-ORD-` + 6-digit sequence → insert `orders` (status `new`), `coupon_redemptions`, `order_items` snapshots, `inventory_movements` (sale) → returns order number + totals.
- `orders` (created in `202609200001_checkout_orders_phase_1.sql`, extended later): `order_number` unique, customer/delivery fields, `payment_method` (`cash_on_delivery` / `bank_transfer`), `status`, `created_at`/`updated_at`, subtotal, delivery fee, Fast surcharge, coupon snapshot + discounts, total (DB check that the total matches its components), `stock_restored_at`. Statuses: **new, confirmed, processing, completed, cancelled** (cancelled is terminal and restores stock). **No payment-status field** — only the payment method is stored. **No tax field.**
- `order_items` snapshots: product title, SKU, variant attributes (RAM, storage, colour, PTA status, condition, warranty), quantity, unit price, line total, delivery scope, image URL → historical prices survive catalog changes.
- RLS: anon/authenticated have no direct table access; `orders` select/update and `order_items` select only for `is_catalog_admin()` (active owner/admin profile). Orders are created only through the SECURITY DEFINER RPC.
- Admin Orders (`src/admin/AdminOrders.tsx`): loads all orders + items once on page open; search; status / payment / city filters; detail panel; status update. No auto-refresh, no per-order URL. Admin dashboard (`src/lib/adminDashboard.ts`) counts `status = 'new'` only on load/Refresh.
- **Not present:** Realtime publication for `orders`, polling, notifications, unread state, invoice/receipt/print/PDF utilities, email/SMTP code. Edge Functions = only `cloudinary-upload-signature` and `cloudinary-delete-media`. Business contact data in code = phone/WhatsApp only (`src/lib/storefrontContact.ts`); no approved business address.

**Roadmap (planning only — the sequence is NOT an authorization):**

| Phase | Scope | Status |
|---|---|---|
| **O1 — Admin new-order notifications** | Frontend-only, no migration: polling (≈60 s + on tab focus), new-order count badge, in-app notification, direct order link (e.g. `/admin/orders?order=ISP-ORD-…`), per-tab "last seen" to avoid duplicate alerts. Browser notifications only while an Admin tab is open — never a guaranteed delivery mechanism. **Owner correction (2026-11-13): `status = new` is NOT equivalent to a notification "unread" state — do not silently treat an order as read when its status changes.** The read/acknowledgement model still needs a decision (a persistent per-admin read state would need a migration) | Planning complete; implementation pending |
| **O2 — Automatic order emails** to `support@isolutionspakistan.com` | Server-side only: proposed outbox table (one row per order, unique `order_id`, status/attempts/last_error/sent_at) filled by an `AFTER INSERT` trigger in the order transaction, sent by a new Edge Function via Supabase Cron/pg_net; retries + duplicate protection; **email failure must never block order creation**; minimal content (no customer phone/address); credentials only as Edge Function secrets set by the Owner. Provider options: Titan SMTP (**NOT verified**) or a free HTTP email API (free-tier limits **NOT verified**; new account + DNS records would need Owner approval) | Feasibility reviewed; provider decision pending; **no migration or integration approved** |
| **O3 — Professional invoice / PDF / Print** | No migration: Admin-only invoice view from stored order snapshots; browser Print / Save as PDF (no paid dependency); `order_number` as reference. **Show the actual payment method only — never invent Paid/Unpaid or tax.** Open Owner decisions: invoice wording ("Order Invoice" / "Order Summary"), business address and contact details to print | Planning complete; implementation pending |
| **O4 — Staff order management** | **Owner decision: Option A — Manual Staff Assignment.** Proposed scope: Owner / authorized Manager manually assigns an order to a staff member; individual secure staff accounts; staff see only their authorized assigned orders; internal notes and processing updates; assignment and activity history; Owner monitors and reassigns; role-based permissions; invoice visibility by authorization. **NOT decided:** staff status-update permissions; which statuses only Owner/Manager control; final roles and access boundaries; detailed operational workflow | Initial Owner decision recorded; **discussion deferred to the next Owner session**; no development or migration authorized |

**Order-management risks (planning):** missed alerts while Admin is closed (O1 + O2 backup); duplicate/failed emails (outbox + unique key + retry limit); customer data in email (minimal content); incorrect Paid/Unpaid labels (none stored); Admin Orders loads all rows and API `max_rows = 1000` (count-only polling, paginate later); DEV/PROD drift; **Supabase CLI linked to PROD — re-link and confirm the project before any future migration**; Cloudflare static hosting is fine for O1/O3 (email runs on Supabase). O4 will need roles/RLS changes (migration) once the Owner decides the details.

### 4M. Order Management — Phase 1 + Phase 2 (2026-11-14 — localhost implemented, DEV migrated, uncommitted)

**Owner decisions (2026-11-14):** Phase 1 = Admin Orders UI + Bulk Actions; Phase 2 = Staff account + mobile-first Staff Orders Dashboard. **Simple shared staff workflow — no individual order assignment** (supersedes the 2026-11-13 O4 Option A plan in §4L). Admin releases an order by marking it **Received**; staff see only released orders. Staff may set In Process → In Transit → Delivered, and Cancelled from an active stage; no backward moves; no Admin approval needed; staff changes show in Admin automatically. **Cancelled-order restore feature = HOLD (not implemented).** Customer accounts, payment gateways, promotions etc. remain unauthorized (AGENTS.md).

**4M.1 Status model (existing values reused, one new value)**

| Display (Admin + Staff) | DB value | Who sets it |
|---|---|---|
| New / Pending | `new` | checkout (`create_storefront_order`, unchanged) |
| **Received** | `confirmed` | Admin — releases the order to staff |
| **In Process** | `processing` | Staff / Admin |
| **In Transit** | `in_transit` (**new**, `202611140002`) | Staff / Admin |
| **Delivered** | `completed` | Staff / Admin |
| Cancelled | `cancelled` | Staff / Admin — final; stock returned exactly once (existing trigger `202610010002`, unchanged) |

Labels live in `ORDER_STATUS_LABELS` / `orderStatusLabel` (`src/lib/orderRules.ts`), used by Admin Orders, the Admin dashboard and `/staff`. Dashboard "open orders" = new, confirmed, processing, in_transit.

**4M.2 Files (all uncommitted)**
- Phase 1 + Admin integration: `src/admin/AdminOrders.tsx`, new `src/admin/adminOrders.css` (all new Orders styles; `admin.css` untouched — it has unrelated uncommitted edits), `src/lib/orderRules.ts` (bulk helpers `planBulkStatusUpdate`, `chunkOrderIds`, `verifyBulkOrderStatusChunk`; labels; staff transitions), `src/admin/AdminDashboard.tsx` + `src/lib/adminDashboard.ts` (labels, open statuses).
- Phase 2: `src/App.tsx` (`/staff` lazy route), new `src/staff/StaffApp.tsx`, `src/staff/staff.css`, `src/lib/staffOrders.ts` (tabs, unseen badge, `tel:` / `wa.me` links with Pakistani number normalisation, error messages).
- Migrations: new `supabase/migrations/202611140001_staff_role.sql`, `supabase/migrations/202611140002_staff_orders_dashboard.sql`.
- Tests: `scripts/checkoutOrders.test.mjs` (2 bulk tests added; updated for In Transit + release RPC), new `scripts/staffOrders.test.mjs` (11 tests).

**4M.3 Database design (`202611140001` + `202611140002`, applied on DEV only)**
- `202611140001`: `alter type public.app_role add value 'staff'` (own file: an enum value cannot be used in the transaction that adds it). `is_catalog_admin()` still accepts only owner/admin, so staff get **no** Admin module access.
- `orders_status_check` + `in_transit`.
- `orders.released_at timestamptz`: trigger `orders_guard_release` (BEFORE UPDATE) sets it the first time an order enters Received / In Process / In Transit (or via `admin_release_orders_to_staff`); once set it never changes or clears; New / Delivered / Cancelled never release. **No backfill:** historical confirmed/processing orders stay hidden from staff until an Admin clicks **Release to staff**.
- `is_order_staff()` (SECURITY DEFINER, active `staff` profile). RLS: `orders_staff_select` (released orders only), `order_items_staff_select` (items of released orders). **No staff UPDATE policy** → a direct staff table update matches zero rows.
- `order_status_events` (append-only history: `status_changed` / `released`, from/to, actor id + role, time), written only by AFTER UPDATE trigger `orders_record_status_event` — covers Admin single, Admin bulk and staff updates. Admin reads all; staff read events of released orders. No direct insert/update/delete.
- `order_staff_seen (order_id, staff_id)` = per-staff seen marker for the badge; staff read only their own rows; written only by RPCs.
- RPCs: `staff_set_order_status(p_order_id, p_status)` (SECURITY DEFINER; active staff only; released order locked `FOR UPDATE`; transitions confirmed→processing/cancelled, processing→in_transit/cancelled, in_transit→completed/cancelled; writes only `status` and a seen row); `staff_mark_order_seen(p_order_id)`; `admin_release_orders_to_staff(p_order_ids uuid[])` (SECURITY INVOKER, Admin check, Admin RLS applies). All revoked from public/anon; new tables `revoke all … from anon, authenticated` then `grant select` (DEV default ACL gives TRUNCATE/TRIGGER/REFERENCES on new tables — removed).

**4M.4 Notifications (free, polling)**
- Staff New Orders badge = released **active** orders this staff account has not opened (`order_staff_seen`); opening an order marks it seen even while it stays Received; status never decides "unread" (Owner QA correction 2026-11-14). In-app banner when new unseen orders arrive. Poll 30 s + on tab focus. No sound / push / paid service.
- Admin "Staff updates N" = staff `status_changed` events newer than the Admin's seen marker (`localStorage` key `isp-admin-staff-updates-seen:<admin id>`, per browser); new staff activity also reloads the Admin list. Poll 60 s + on focus. First visit starts from "now".

**4M.5 Verification (2026-11-14)**
- Local (PGlite replay of all migrations, hosted default privileges): `staffOrders.test.mjs` 11/11 + `checkoutOrders.test.mjs` 26/26 = **37/37 PASS** — staff see only released orders/items; no access to inventory, coupons, coupon redemptions, contact messages, other profiles, stock levels; inactive staff / customers / anon see nothing; staff direct writes (status, totals, customer fields, delete, history, seen, items, stock) all fail; forward-only transitions; Delivered/Cancelled read-only for staff; staff cancel from each active stage restores stock once; per-staff seen; historical orders not released by the migration; Admin bulk In Transit releases + one history row per order; checkout still creates `new` orders.
- `tsc -b`, eslint (changed files), `npm run build:dev` PASS; StaffApp chunk ≈ 16.6 kB JS + 9 kB CSS; `/staff` loads no Admin code.
- Mock-data harness UI QA (scratchpad, no network/DB): Admin desktop + mobile (bulk cancel, partial result with an order cancelled meanwhile, chips/labels, Not released → Release to staff → history, Staff updates badge + Mark all seen, bulk In Transit) and Staff mobile 375 px + desktop 1280 px (badge, open = seen, Received → In Process → In Transit → Delivered → History, Cancel confirm, new-order banner, no horizontal scroll).
- DEV read-only preflight + post-check: see §0. Pre-migration fingerprints (md5): orders `1a2b4669…`, items `cba06010…`, movements `25caa89e…` — identical after.
- **Not verified:** real `/staff` login, real Admin release on DEV, real staff status changes, real-phone UI. Localhost `http://127.0.0.1:5173/staff` login page renders against DEV; signing in needs a Staff account.

**4M.6 Localhost**
- `npm run dev` → `http://127.0.0.1:5173/admin/orders` and `http://127.0.0.1:5173/staff` (`.env.local` = DEV `acwatgxkcyrdxpcljxjb`). The DEV schema now matches the code, so In Transit, release and history work on localhost.
- Admin and Staff share one Supabase session per browser (same origin) — use a separate browser/phone for staff.

**4M.7 Known limitations**
- Polling, not realtime (badge delay up to 30–60 s). Staff list loads all released orders (API `max_rows` 1000; pagination later). Admin "staff updates seen" is per browser. Staff list has no search yet.
- Cloudflare Worker SPA fallback for `/staff` must be checked at deploy time (Firebase rewrites `**` → `/index.html` already).
- `securityRls.test.mjs` authenticated RPC allowlist needs a reviewed update before commit (§0, §13 #76).

**4M.8 Rollback considerations (not executed)**
- DEV forward fix preferred. Manual rollback of `202611140002` would drop the two triggers + functions, the staff policies, `order_status_events`, `order_staff_seen`, the RPCs and `released_at`, and restore the old status constraint (only if no order is `in_transit`). The `staff` enum value cannot be removed simply — harmless if left. `202611090001` rollback is complex (search function redefined) — keep forward.

**4M.9 Next steps (each needs separate Owner approval, in this order)**
1. **DEV Staff Test Account (next session start).** Owner: DEV Supabase Dashboard → Authentication → Add user (email + strong password set by the Owner; Claude never sees it). The `on_auth_user_created` trigger creates a profile with role NULL. Then in DEV SQL Editor: `update public.profiles set role = 'staff', is_active = true where id = '<new user id>';`. Claude can then read-only verify (`is_order_staff`, profile count). Disable later with `is_active = false` (optionally ban the user).

### 4N. Two-Vendor Order Management (2026-11-14 — localhost implemented, migrations as files only, uncommitted)

**Owner approval (2026-11-14):** two vendors — `isolutions` (iSolutions Pakistan: mobile phones, mobile accessories, tablets, iPads, MacBooks, Watches/Bands and every non-Piyavey item) and `piyavey` (Piyavey: Home Gadgets only). Admin assigns each order item; one order may contain both vendors; each vendor has its own staff accounts and sees only its own items; both use the existing `/staff` dashboard. Decisions **D1** all items assigned before release, no default vendor, Assign All shortcut · **D2** staff may cancel their whole vendor part · **D3** only Admin reassigns after release, only while the affected parts are Received · **D4** item-level cancellation inside a part deferred (not implemented) · **D5** staff see only their item subtotal; no COD/payment split, fee allocation or checkout change · **D6** parent = earliest active stage, partial cancellation shown · **D7** split orders managed per vendor; bulk actions cannot bypass · **D8** seen/unseen per staff account. Authorised: localhost code, migration files, local tests only.

**4N.1 Data model (`202611150002`)**
- `vendors` (2 seeded rows; Admin SELECT only; changed only by migration). `profiles.vendor_id` + check `profiles_vendor_matches_role`: role `staff` ⇔ vendor set (no staff existed on DEV/PROD, so nothing to backfill).
- `order_items.vendor_id` (nullable; set only by `admin_assign_order_items`; `create_storefront_order` untouched → new orders stay unassigned).
- `order_fulfilments` (one row per order + vendor; status confirmed / processing / in_transit / completed / cancelled; `released_at`, `stock_restored_at`; unique (order, vendor); Admin SELECT only; no client writes). Its items = order items with the same vendor. Rows are never deleted; a part emptied by reassignment stays as a record but is ignored (not "live").
- `order_status_events` + `fulfilment_id`, `vendor_id`, `from_vendor_id`, `order_item_id`, new event `item_reassigned`. `order_fulfilment_seen (fulfilment_id, staff_id)` — RPC-only.
- Triggers: `order_fulfilments_guard` (insert = Received/now; order/vendor/release immutable; cancelled final; **cancel returns only this vendor's lines, net of earlier returns**, stamps `stock_restored_at` once) · `order_fulfilments_after_change` (vendor history event + parent sync) · `orders_guard_split_status` (a split order's status can only equal the value derived from its live parts → direct/bulk Admin updates of split orders fail with `split_order_status_managed_per_vendor`; non-status edits and orders without parts unchanged).
- Parent derivation (`derive_order_status_from_fulfilments`, mirrored by `deriveOrderStatus` in `src/lib/vendorOrders.ts`): all live parts cancelled → Cancelled; no active part left → Delivered (incl. Delivered + Cancelled); otherwise earliest of Received / In Process / In Transit.

**4N.2 Stock (`202611150001` + fulfilment trigger)**
- `202611150001` changes only `restore_cancelled_order_stock`: it now nets `sale` **and** `return` movements per variant and returns only what is still out. For every order without an earlier return (all historical orders) the result is identical to before. Cancelled-final, `stock_restored_at` once and the unique index `inventory_movements_order_return_once` are unchanged and remain the hard once-per-variant guarantee (one variant appears once per order, so once per item).
- Vendor cancel returns only that vendor's variants; a later whole-order cancel (Admin "Cancel all open vendor parts", or the parent becoming Cancelled) returns only the rest; repeated cancels return nothing. All RPCs lock the parent order row first, then the part, so vendor/Admin/concurrent cancels serialise.

**4N.3 Security**
- Phase 2 order-level staff access removed: policies `orders_staff_select`, `order_items_staff_select`, `order_status_events_staff_select`, `order_staff_seen_own_select` dropped; RPCs `staff_set_order_status`, `staff_mark_order_seen`, `admin_release_orders_to_staff` dropped. Staff now have **no direct table access** to orders, items, parts, history, seen markers, vendors or stock.
- Staff RPCs (SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path=''`, active staff of an active vendor via `current_staff_vendor()`): `is_order_staff`, `staff_current_vendor`, `staff_list_fulfilments` (own released live parts: customer contact/delivery, payment + shipping method, own items, `vendor_subtotal_minor`, seen flag — **no** whole-order totals, fees, coupons or other vendors' lines), `staff_fulfilment_history`, `staff_mark_fulfilment_seen`, `staff_set_fulfilment_status` (forward Received → In Process → In Transit → Delivered; Cancelled from any active stage; own vendor only).
- Admin RPCs (refuse non-admins): `admin_assign_order_items` (all-or-nothing; before release free incl. unassign; after release D3), `admin_release_order_to_vendors` (New/Received only, refuses `order_items_unassigned`), `admin_set_fulfilment_status` (any change except leaving Cancelled), `admin_cancel_open_fulfilments` (Delivered parts kept). All revoked from public/anon; new tables `revoke all … from anon, authenticated` then SELECT only where needed.

**4N.4 UI**
- Admin Orders (`src/admin/AdminOrders.tsx` + new `src/admin/AdminOrderVendors.tsx`, styles in `adminOrders.css`): per-item vendor select with "Suggested: …" (`suggestVendor`: category `gadgets` → Piyavey except `ACCESSORIES_EXTRA_PRODUCT_IDS` → iSolutions; unknown category → no suggestion), **Use suggestions**, **Assign all to…**, **Save vendors**, **Release to vendors** (disabled while unsaved or unassigned); after release: one card per vendor (items, subtotal, status select, "stock returned"), item move (only between Received parts), **Cancel all open vendor parts** (confirmation). Split orders: order status read-only ("follows the vendor parts") + **Partially cancelled** chip; list shows "Not released" / "N vendors" / "Partially cancelled"; bulk actions skip split orders (`planBulkStatusUpdate` → `skippedVendor`); history labels vendor events; Staff updates = vendor staff changes with vendor name. Without the migration the panel says it is unavailable and the legacy view still loads.
- Staff (`src/staff/StaffApp.tsx`): same layout/workflow, Call/WhatsApp, Active/History, badge, banner, 30 s polling; data from `staff_list_fulfilments`; header shows the vendor name; payment block shows **Your items subtotal** only.

**4N.5 Files** — new: `supabase/migrations/202611150001_order_cancel_restore_net.sql`, `supabase/migrations/202611150002_vendor_order_fulfilments.sql`, `src/lib/vendorOrders.ts`, `src/admin/AdminOrderVendors.tsx`, `scripts/vendorOrders.test.mjs`. Changed (already uncommitted files): `src/admin/AdminOrders.tsx`, `src/admin/adminOrders.css`, `src/staff/StaffApp.tsx`, `src/lib/orderRules.ts`, `src/lib/staffOrders.ts`, `scripts/staffOrders.test.mjs` (Phase 2 tests moved to the vendor model), `scripts/checkoutOrders.test.mjs`, `scripts/securityRls.test.mjs` (allowlist). No change to checkout, shipping, payment, catalog or any applied migration file.

**4N.6 Verification (2026-11-14, local only)**
- `scripts/vendorOrders.test.mjs` 15/15 (PGlite replay of all 72 migrations, hosted default privileges): vendor seed + staff vendor rule; checkout unchanged; category suggestions incl. all 16 Watches/Bands; D1 unassigned release refused + Admin-only + bad input refused all-or-nothing; single-vendor order; mixed order (own items/subtotal only, no whole-order financial keys); cross-vendor + direct table read/write denial, inactive staff/vendor denied, old RPCs gone; independent transitions + parent derivation + vendor history; Admin per-vendor override; direct/bulk split updates refused, legacy bulk works; D3 reassignment (emptied part hidden, kept, seen reset, In Process locks both directions, staff cannot reassign); partial cancel (only that vendor's stock, once; other vendor delivers → Delivered + partial); subsequent full cancel returns only the rest, legacy whole cancel unchanged, unique index still blocks a 2nd return; simultaneous cancels (vendor+vendor, vendor+Admin) → one return per line; seen per account/part; legacy Phase-2-released orders invisible to vendors, legacy In Process not releasable.
- `staffOrders.test.mjs` 8/8, `checkoutOrders.test.mjs` 26/26, `securityRls.test.mjs` PASS. Full suite 1256/1257 (unrelated `phase4` failure). `tsc -b`, eslint, `build:dev` PASS.
- Mock-data UI harness (scratchpad, no network/DB): Admin 375 px — suggestions (kettle → Piyavey, Samsung Watch 7 → iSolutions, cable → iSolutions), save, release blocked until saved/assigned, release → two vendor cards; Piyavey staff 375 px — only its own item and Rs 4,500 subtotal, no whole-order total; cancel own part → iSolutions part unaffected; Admin — Received + Partially cancelled, Staff updates badge, vendor history; desktop — D3 options disabled when a part is In Process, bulk dialog skips the split order. No horizontal scroll at 375 px.
- **Not verified:** real DEV database, real logins, real phones. True parallel sessions cannot run in PGlite (single connection); concurrency safety rests on the order-row lock + unique index + tests of interleaved calls.

**4N.7 Known limitations / risks**
- Watches/Bands exception is by product ID (`ACCESSORIES_EXTRA_PRODUCT_IDS`, PROD IDs); a future watch added to `gadgets` is suggested as Piyavey until added to that list — the Admin confirms every suggestion.
- D5: staff see no amount to collect; COD collection for mixed orders stays an Owner/Admin process. Order totals never change on partial cancellation.
- `order_notes` (customer note) and customer email are shown to both vendors (delivery information).
- Admin "Staff updates" seen marker is still per browser; polling (30/60 s), not realtime.
- After the migration, Phase 2 order-level "Received = visible to staff" no longer applies: staff see an order only after vendor release. `order_staff_seen` remains as an unused legacy table.
- Rollback of `202611150002` is not simple once parts exist (forward fix preferred); `202611150001` is a function body change (reversible by re-creating the previous body).

**4N.8 DEV/PROD**: nothing applied. DEV is at `202611140002` (70); PROD at `202611090001` (68). The new code **requires** `202611150002` for vendor features; the Admin page falls back to the legacy view without it, but the Staff Dashboard needs it. Never deploy this build to a database without both new migrations.

**4N.9 Next steps (each needs separate Owner approval, in order)**
1. Owner review of this implementation (optionally the mock harness).
2. DEV read-only preflight (orders/status counts, staff profiles = 0, migration list) → apply `202611150001` + `202611150002` to DEV (`--remote=acwatgxkcyrdxpcljxjb`; CLI stays linked to PROD) → read-only post-verification.
3. Owner creates DEV staff users (one per vendor) and sets `update public.profiles set role = 'staff', vendor_id = (select id from public.vendors where code = '<isolutions|piyavey>'), is_active = true where id = '<user id>';`.
4. DEV test orders (single + mixed) → real Admin/Staff QA on localhost with two browsers/phones.
5. Commit (temporary Git index; only Order Management entries), push, DEV deploy, PROD migrations `202611140001`, `202611140002`, `202611150001`, `202611150002` together with a PROD deploy — each approved.

### 4O. Staff Order Email Notifications (2026-11-15 — PROD DEPLOYED; PROD inbox delivery pending — see 4O.8; 4O.1–4O.7 are the historical localhost record)

**4O.1 Status (historical, superseded by 4O.8).** Owner-approved development, **localhost only**. Nothing applied to DEV/PROD, nothing deployed, nothing committed. Actual email delivery **NOT verified** (no provider integrated).

**4O.2 Recipients (Owner-provided).** iSolutions Pakistan → `ordersisolution@gmail.com`; Piyavey → `isolutionspiyave@gmail.com`. Stored as `vendors.notification_email` by the migration. A mixed order sends one separate email per vendor.

**4O.3 Design (outbox pattern).**
- Migration **`202611160001_vendor_order_notifications.sql`** (file only): `vendors.notification_email`; table **`vendor_order_notifications`** (one row per vendor fulfilment, **UNIQUE `fulfilment_id`**, status `pending/sending/sent/failed`, attempts, `next_attempt_at`, lease, `last_error`, `sent_at`); RLS on, Admin-only select, no write grants.
- An **AFTER INSERT trigger on `order_fulfilments`** queues the email inside the release transaction: a failed release queues nothing; a repeated release cannot queue a second email. Enqueue errors are caught (WARNING only) → **never block Confirm & Release**. A vendor without a mailbox gets no row.
- **Content snapshot:** order number, vendor name, ONLY that vendor's items (title, SKU, variant, quantity) + Staff Dashboard link `https://isolutionspakistan.com/staff`. **No prices/totals, no customer name/phone/address/email/notes, never the other vendor's items.**
- Worker RPCs (SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path = ''`, Admin session or service role only): `admin_claim_vendor_notifications` (lease due rows, `skip locked`), `admin_complete_vendor_notification` (lease must match), `admin_retry_vendor_notification` (Admin re-queues a `failed` row; a `sent` row is never re-sent).
- **Retry:** backoff 1, 5, 15, 60, 240 min; max 6 attempts → `failed`; a crashed send loses its 5-min lease and becomes due again (at-least-once; lease id = provider idempotency key).
- Edge Function **`vendor-order-notifications`** (not deployed): runs with the Admin's JWT (`requireCatalogAdmin`, no service-role key), drains all due rows, one bad row never stops the others. **No provider transport implemented:** until `VENDOR_EMAIL_PROVIDER` names an implemented transport it returns `{ configured: false }` **without claiming** (queued rows wait untouched). Credentials will be Edge Function secrets only, never frontend/Git.
- Admin Orders (`AdminOrderVendors.tsx`): after release the order reload triggers a **best-effort, non-blocking** delivery call; each vendor part shows *Email queued / sent / retrying / failed* + **Retry email**. Errors (table missing, function not deployed) only hide the line.

**4O.4 Files (uncommitted).** `supabase/migrations/202611160001_vendor_order_notifications.sql`, `supabase/functions/vendor-order-notifications/index.ts`, `supabase/functions/_shared/vendorNotificationEmail.ts`, `src/lib/vendorNotifications.ts`, `src/admin/AdminOrderVendors.tsx`, `src/admin/adminOrders.css`, `scripts/vendorNotifications.test.mjs`, `scripts/checkoutOrders.test.mjs`, `scripts/securityRls.test.mjs`.

**4O.5 Verification (Claude-reported, localhost).** 14 new tests: iSolutions-only, Piyavey-only, mixed release, duplicate-release protection, failure + backoff + give-up + re-queue, lease expiry, cross-vendor privacy, enqueue failure / missing mailbox never blocks release, failed release queues nothing, access control, rendering safety (header injection, HTML escaping). Order tests 79/79; full suite 1279/1280 (unrelated `phase4` failure); `tsc -b`, ESLint, `build:prod` PASS. Mock harness (port 5221): release → *Email sent* / *Email failed* → Retry → *Email sent*, no console errors.

**4O.6 Provider decision — PENDING (Owner).** Gmail preferred; existing `support@isolutionspakistan.com` available; no new sender address approved. Open questions: Gmail API (OAuth/refresh token) vs Gmail SMTP (app password, 2-Step Verification) vs Titan SMTP (`smtpout.secureserver.net`:465 per GoDaddy help; automated sending/limits NOT verified); whether Supabase Edge Functions can reach SMTP ports (no official statement found; must be tested from a deployed function); HTTP APIs work over 443. **Do not** create accounts, set credentials/secrets, change DNS or activate sending without Owner approval.

**4O.7 Rollout order (each step separately approved).** Provider decision → transport implementation (localhost) → DEV migration `202611160001` (+ DEV catch-up `202611150001/2/3`) + Edge Function deploy + secrets (Owner) → DEV test release → commit → PROD migration + Edge Function + frontend deploy. Until the migration is applied, the deployed frontend simply shows no email line.

**4O.8 Rollout record (2026-11-15, Owner-approved step by step; VERIFIED unless marked).**
- **Provider decided:** Gmail API (OAuth client + refresh token, scope `gmail.send` only), sender `ordersisolution@gmail.com`. Credentials only in Edge Function secrets and the private local file `C:\Users\User\isolutions-gmail-secrets.env` (never in Git, frontend, logs or chat). ### 4P. Gmail Order Notifications — final state (2026-11-17 — Admin New Order Alert + retry scheduler PROD DEPLOYED; VERIFIED unless marked)

**4P.1 Commits.** Commit A **`46c9958`** "feat: Vendor Gmail order notifications (Confirm & Release emails, Admin status + Retry)" — 12 files, the code deployed to PROD on 2026-11-15. Commit B **`4a11c9f`** "feat: Admin New Order Gmail Alert with automatic retry scheduler" — 10 files: migrations `202611170001_admin_new_order_notifications.sql` + `202611180001_admin_order_notification_retry_schedule.sql`, `supabase/functions/admin-order-notifications/index.ts`, `supabase/functions/_shared/adminOrderNotificationEmail.ts`, `src/lib/adminOrderNotifications.ts`, the alert-only hunks of `src/StorefrontApp.tsx` + `src/admin/AdminOrders.tsx`, `scripts/adminOrderNotifications.test.mjs`, the alert hunks of `scripts/securityRls.test.mjs`, and pg_cron/pg_net/Vault stand-ins in `scripts/support/catalogTestDatabase.mjs`. Both made with a temporary Git index; unrelated entries untouched. **Not pushed** (25 local commits).

**4P.2 Addresses (Owner-approved).**
- **Sender (all emails):** `ordersisolution@gmail.com` — Gmail API, OAuth refresh token, scope `gmail.send` only.
- **Admin New Order Alert:** `isolutionpakistan89@gmail.com` (hard-coded in migration `202611170001`; change only by a reviewed migration).
- **Vendor iSolutions Pakistan:** `ordersisolution@gmail.com` (`vendors.notification_email`).
- **Vendor Piyavey:** `isolutionspiyave@gmail.com` (`vendors.notification_email`).

**4P.3 When emails are sent.**
- **Admin alert:** a DEFERRED constraint trigger on `orders` queues one row in `admin_order_notifications` at COMMIT of the checkout transaction (after all order items exist; a rolled-back checkout queues nothing; UNIQUE `order_id`). Delivery: (1) the storefront fires a fire-and-forget `keepalive` POST right after a successful checkout (normally delivered within seconds — DEV test: 2 s); (2) opening Admin Orders triggers a backup drain; (3) **pg_cron job `admin-order-notifications-retry` every minute** calls `public.dispatch_admin_order_notifications()`, which makes NO HTTP call when nothing is due and otherwise one POST (via pg_net) to the Edge Function. `create_storefront_order` was NOT modified.
- **Vendor emails:** only after **Confirm & Release** — one separate email per vendor part (trigger on `order_fulfilments`), no prices or customer details. Delivery is triggered from Admin Orders (after release / reload) with the Admin's session; Admin sees per-vendor email status + **Retry email**. The pg_cron job does **not** drain vendor emails.

**4P.4 Retry behaviour (both outboxes).** Backoff 1, 5, 15, 60, 240 min; max 6 attempts → `failed`. A 5-minute lease protects each send; a crashed send becomes due again (at-least-once — a rare duplicate email is possible if Gmail accepted a message but the result was not recorded). `skip locked` leasing means concurrent drains (storefront + Admin + scheduler) never send the same row twice. A `sent` row is never sent again. Admin alerts have **no Admin UI status/Retry** (status is readable in the table by Admin); a `failed` admin alert needs a reviewed DB action to re-queue.

**4P.5 Security boundaries.**
- Gmail credentials exist only as Edge Function secrets (`GMAIL_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`, `GMAIL_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`, `GMAIL_OAUTH_REFRESH_TOKEN`, `GMAIL_SENDER_ADDRESS`) and in the private local file `C:\Users\User\isolutions-gmail-secrets.env` — never in Git, frontend, logs, chat or these documents.
- `admin-order-notifications` is public (`--no-verify-jwt`, needed by anonymous checkout) but takes **no input**: it only drains rows the database queued, so a caller cannot choose recipient, content or order. It uses the service role **inside the function only** (injected by Supabase).
- Worker RPCs (`admin_has_due_…`, `admin_claim_…`, `admin_complete_…`) accept only the service role or an Admin session; the dispatcher is executable by nobody except the scheduler (revoked from anon, authenticated, service_role); the Vault URL must match the exact `https://<ref>.supabase.co/functions/v1/admin-order-notifications` pattern or no call is made; the scheduler sends `{}` with no credentials.
- RLS on both outboxes: Admin-only select, no write grants; anon has no access.
- The Admin alert contains order number, Pakistan time, payment method, shipping method, items (title, SKU, variant, quantity, prices) and amounts — **no customer name, phone, email, address, city or notes**.

**4P.6 Emergency off switches (checkout keeps working in every case).**
- Admin alert: delete secret `ADMIN_ORDER_EMAIL_PROVIDER` → the function returns `configured:false` without claiming; rows wait; scheduler calls stay harmless. To stop the scheduler itself: `select cron.unschedule('admin-order-notifications-retry')` (database write — Owner approval).
- Vendor emails: delete secret `VENDOR_EMAIL_PROVIDER`.
- Frontend: Cloudflare rollback to `a0a36ed7-74bc-4891-932b-771ab84e3d72`.

### 4R.1 Agent checklist — iPhone Stock Update data (mandatory / optional; VERIFIED against code 2026-11-19)

Page: Admin Studio → **iPhone Stock Update** (`/admin/iphone-stock-update`; parser `src/lib/iphoneStockUpdate.ts`). Same rules for New and Used.

| Information | Status | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Model (e.g. iPhone 15 Pro Max) | **Mandatory** | Pasted text (heading, or one-line form) |
| Storage (e.g. 256GB) | **Mandatory** | Pasted text (heading, or `256Gb @ price` line in the supplier block form) |
| Colour | **Mandatory** | Pasted text; unknown spelling → Needs Attention (Owner keeps or fixes) |
| Price | **Mandatory** | Pasted text (`@298,000`, `298,000`, `Rs 298,000/-`) |
| New / Used | **Mandatory** | Page choice, or the word `New`/`Used` in the text (text wins; never a default) |
| PTA Approved / Non-PTA | **Mandatory** | Page choice, or `PTA`/`Non-PTA` in the text (text wins; never a default; `HK Approved` = note only, never PTA) |
| SIM | Optional | Only `Physical Dual` → Dual Physical SIM; blank = NULL; must equal an existing variant's stored SIM |
| Warranty | **Not read** | Always NULL on new variants; existing warranties never touched; supplier warranty wording → "Lines not understood" (nothing saved) |
| RAM / grade / battery / cycles | **Not read** | Always NULL; a RAM size in the heading → Needs Attention |
| Quantity | **Not read** | New variant / new phone = stock 20; existing variant keeps its stock |

- **Needs Attention holds the whole phone** (nothing written for it; other phones still update): missing New/Used or PTA, unknown colour, colour line without a heading, conflicting words (New + Used, PTA + Non-PTA), duplicate variant in the list, `JV`, look-alike existing phone (Owner confirms "This is a new phone"), price not below compare-at, SIM mismatch, product with variants of the other condition.
- **Skipped:** a price without a colour. **Not touched:** variants absent from the list. **New phones** are created as **drafts** (Karachi only).
- **Matching:** exact title only — New `Apple iPhone 15 Pro Max`, Used `Apple iPhone 15 Pro Max (Used)`.
- **Simple working example** (page: New + PTA Approved): `iPhone 15 Pro Max 256GB` / `Blue @ 298,000` / `Black @ 298,000` / `White @ 300,000`.
- **Publishing (since 2026-11-19, DEV + PROD):** warranty is optional for New and Used iPhones; PTA, condition, price, delivery, active variant and primary image remain required.

### 4S. Simple Website Editor (planned 2026-11-18; Banner & Slider module LOCALHOST COMPLETE 2026-11-20 — deployment HOLD; other modules NOT started)

- **Owner direction:** a **Simple Website Editor** inside Admin Studio — **not** a Shopify clone, no unrestricted drag-and-drop canvas, no custom HTML/JS/CSS, no theme marketplace, no AI generation. Existing website design is the default; product, price, stock, PTA/condition/warranty facts, checkout, shipping, orders, Gmail notifications and inventory are never touched. **Editor access: Owner only.**
- **Required features:** Announcement Bar (messages add/remove/order, text, background incl. gradient, colours, speed, direction, links, show/hide; current animation + accessibility preserved); Homepage Banner Manager (desktop + mobile images, add/replace/remove/reorder, enable/disable, optional heading/button/link; current carousel behaviour preserved); existing homepage sections (headings/descriptions, supported images/colours, show/hide, reorder); **+ Add Section** with six templates — Image + Text, Promotional Banners, Product Collection, Text Section, Category Cards, Special Offer (display-only); desktop/mobile preview; Save Draft / Publish / Restore; safe fallback to today's site.
- **Read-only findings (VERIFIED in code 2026-11-18):** announcement bar messages + styles hardcoded (`src/StorefrontApp.tsx` Header, `src/styles.css` ticker blocks; 36 s loop, hover pause, reduced-motion); hero = 7 hardcoded slides from `/assets/banners` (desktop 2000×839, mobile 1122×1402); homepage section order fixed in `Home()`; Admin → Homepage = Bundle Manager only; Bundle Offers shows 4 demo images because `USE_LIVE_BUNDLES = false`; Featured / Flash Sale / Best Seller come from product flags.
- **Master Plan (four phases, each localhost → DEV → PROD with Owner approval at every step):** shared architecture defined up front — tables `site_content_versions` (scopes `announcement_bar` + `homepage`; one draft + one published per scope; history) and `site_assets` (Cloudinary `…/site/<uuid>`); RLS; public read via one published-only RPC; draft/publish/restore RPCs (publish/restore Owner-only); server-side content validation (hex colours, contrast, link allowlist, lengths, counts); built-in defaults = today's content as fallback + kill switch. Phase 1 Foundation + Announcement Bar (migration M1 = full schema); Phase 2 Banner Manager (M2 = validator replacement only + Edge Function `site` upload/delete target); Phase 3 Existing Sections (M3 = validator replacement only); Phase 4 Add Section templates (no migration). No new libraries (up/down reorder buttons, no drag-and-drop library).
- **Pending Owner decisions:** (1) Bundle Offers — keep demo tiles as an editable section vs switch to live bundles; (2) external link allowlist beyond site pages / WhatsApp / phone; (3) visible pause button on the announcement bar for mobile. (Draft-editing access is settled by the Owner direction: Owner only.)

### 4T. iPhone Model-Based Shared Specifications (2026-11-20 — LOCALHOST COMPLETE; migration `202611200002` NOT applied)

> **2026-11-21:** extended to iPhone + Android with approval, correction and automatic sharing, and merged into Spec Upload as the **Same Model** tab — see **§4U**. The RPC is now `apply_model_common_specifications` (the 2026-11-20 `apply_iphone_model_specifications` text below is historical).

- **Owner rule:** same iPhone model = same common specifications. PTA Approved / Non-PTA, New / Used, colour, storage and "Apple" in the title make no difference.
- **Root cause (VERIFIED):** specifications are stored per product (`product_specifications`), Spec Upload matches one exact product per row, and the PDP reads only that product's rows, so nothing linked products of the same model. Read-only PROD check: `Apple iPhone 18 Pro Max` (PTA Approved, published) **29** specs; `iPhone 18 Pro Max` (Non-PTA, published, slug `apple-iphone`) **0**.
- **Approach (reuses the existing architecture):** canonical model key from the title (`public.iphone_model_key` mirrored by `src/lib/iphoneModelSpecs.ts`): `Apple ` prefix, ` (Used)` suffix, case/spacing, `15Pro` / `ProMax` ignored; Pro vs Pro Max and generations never merged; extra words (storage, colour, JV, SE, unknown suffixes) = ambiguous, listed and never matched; only Apple + Mobile Phones. The model's specs are added to each matching product's **own** rows, so the PDP and Spec Upload are unchanged. Preview reuses `planSpecificationUpload` (New / Already Present / Conflict).
- **Write path:** `apply_iphone_model_specifications(model_key, product_ids, specs)` (SECURITY INVOKER) — Owner only (`is_catalog_owner`), server recomputes the exact product set and refuses a stale one, add-only (existing label any section: same value = present, different = conflict kept), never updates/deletes, refuses variant facts (storage, RAM, colour, price, PTA, condition, warranty, battery health/cycles, grade, SIM, stock, IMEI), all or nothing. No table, product, variant, title or slug change.
- **Admin:** `/admin/iphone-model-specs` (nav "iPhone Model Specs"): 1 choose model, 2 give specs once (copy from a product of the model or a Section/Label/Value CSV/XLSX), 3 preview (counts + per-product condition, PTA, status, add/present/conflicts), 4 typed confirmation. Until the migration is applied, saving is refused with "Nothing was saved".
- **Files:** `src/lib/iphoneModelSpecs.ts`, `src/lib/iphoneModelSpecsDb.ts`, `src/admin/IphoneModelSpecs.tsx`, `src/admin/AdminApp.tsx`, `src/admin/admin.css`, `supabase/migrations/202611200002_iphone_model_shared_specifications.sql`, tests `scripts/iphoneModelSpecs.test.mjs` (+ `securityRls` allowlist: `is_catalog_owner`).
- **QA (VERIFIED):** 11/11 focused (27 title cases identical in TS and SQL; Pro vs Pro Max; ambiguous blocked; Admin / signed-out / anon refused; stale set refused; conflicts kept; idempotent; all variant data, titles and slugs unchanged); localhost visual QA in an offline Admin harness with a read-only copy of published PROD iPhone titles/specs (+3 test-only products): iPhone 18 Pro Max preview correct, save refused while not installed.
- **Pending (each separately approved):** (1) **accuracy verification of the existing 29 specs** (e.g. chipset / OS values) against an official source before they are shared; (2) **Owner decision: automatic sharing for future iPhones** — today a product added later needs the model page re-run (only missing specs are added); (3) DEV apply `202611200002` (explicit DEV ref) -> DEV QA -> PROD; (4) focused commit.

### 4U. 2026-11-21 work (ALL LOCALHOST ONLY — nothing applied, committed or deployed; VERIFIED unless marked)

#### 4U.1 Same Model Specifications — iPhone + Android (migration `202611200002` edited, NOT applied)
- **Owner rule:** same model = same common specifications; future products of the model receive the Owner-approved list automatically; unverified specs never propagate; existing specs never overwritten without approval; different models never mix.
- **Model key** (`src/lib/iphoneModelSpecs.ts` mirrors SQL `public.model_title_core`, `public.iphone_model_key`, `public.model_brand_series_alias`, `public.model_spec_key`): Mobile Phones only. Ignored: PTA / PTA Approved / Non-PTA (also in brackets), storage and RAM+storage, trailing `(Used)`, a trailing known colour (last word must be a colour; modifiers like Natural/Cosmic/Deep may precede it), brand prefix, series alias `galaxy` (Samsung) / `moto` (Motorola). Kept apart: Pro, Pro+, Plus, Max, Ultra, Lite, FE, 5G, mini, e. Any other bracket = no key (never matched). iPhone keys `iphone-<n>[-variant]`; Android keys `<brand-slug>-<model>` (e.g. `samsung-a57`, `oppo-reno-15-pro-5g`, `honor-x5c-plus`). Unknown colours (e.g. "Silverblue") become a separate model (never mixed, just not matched).
- **Database (file only):** new table `public.model_common_specifications` (model_key, section, label, value, sort_order, approved_by, approved_at; unique (model_key, lower(label)); RLS: Admin read, no direct writes). RPC **`apply_model_common_specifications(p_model_key, p_product_ids, p_specs, p_corrections)`** — SECURITY DEFINER, **Owner only**, server recomputes the exact product set (stale set refused), refuses variant facts, records the approved list (add-only), adds missing specs to every product (add-only), all or nothing. **Corrections** (`p_corrections` = label + old approved value): replaces the approved value and updates only products whose stored value equals the old approved value; stale old value → everything refused. **Deferred constraint trigger** `products_copy_approved_model_specifications` (after insert or update of title/brand/category) copies missing **approved** specs to new or renamed products of the model; the product's own specs (e.g. from Bulk Import) are saved first and win. The old `apply_iphone_model_specifications` is dropped in the same file (it was never applied).
- **Admin UI** (`src/admin/IphoneModelSpecs.tsx`, shown as the Same Model tab §4U.2): models grouped iPhone / Android; ambiguous titles listed; source = copy from a product of the model or a Section/Label/Value CSV/XLSX; "Approved specifications (CSV)" download for corrections; preview (matching products, additions, present, conflicts, approved values to correct with tick boxes); "I verified these specifications" tick + typed phrase `APPROVE <n> SPECS AND ADD <m>[ AND CORRECT <c>]`. Until the migration is applied, saving is refused ("Nothing was saved").
- **Not done / risks:** real DEV/PROD behaviour not tested (in-memory replay only); the 29 existing iPhone 18 Pro Max specs must be verified before approval; aliases beyond Galaxy/Moto (e.g. Redmi/Poco) not added (need Owner approval); colour list is fixed.

#### 4U.7 Same Model Specifications — final approval workflow (Owner decision; latest; wins over §4U.1 where it differs)
- **Business rule (Owner):** (1) Owner gives the specification Excel/CSV to ChatGPT; (2) ChatGPT verifies against official sources and prepares the final sheet; (3) that final verified sheet is **already APPROVED**; (4) no individual spec verification, checkbox or typed approval from the Owner; (5) after the sheet is uploaded in Admin → Spec Upload → Same Model, existing and future exact-matching iPhone/Android products get the approved common specs; (6) existing product specs are never overwritten, corrections and conflicts handled safely; (7) unverified or ambiguous specs are never applied automatically; (8) the upload stays under Owner control — choosing a file never writes.
- **Admin (`src/admin/IphoneModelSpecs.tsx`, same page/route):** step 2 = "Upload the final verified sheet" (.csv/.xlsx; Template + Approved-specs CSV downloads kept); step 3 preview (matching products, add / present / conflicts, approved values corrected by the sheet — listed, no tick, skipped rows with reasons); step 4 = **"Apply approved sheet to N products"** + one `window.confirm` summary. Removed: "I verified…" checkbox, typed `APPROVE <n> SPECS AND ADD <m>` phrase, per-correction checkboxes, "Copy from a product of this model" source. `chooseFile` only parses; `applyIphoneModelSpecs` is called once, from `save()`.
- **Lib (`src/lib/iphoneModelSpecs.ts`):** optional **Status** column (`Status` / `Verification` / `Verified`) read into `ModelSpec.status`; new `approvedSheetRows(specs)` → `{ apply, skipped }` — skipped: Status present and not Verified/Approved/Official/Confirmed/Final/OK/Yes/✓, blank value, placeholder value (`-`, `N/A`, `TBD`, `TBC`, `unknown`, `unverified`, `pending`, `to be confirmed`, any `?`), variant facts. `planModelSpecs(..., corrections = "all")` — the sheet corrects every differing approved value (a Set still limits it). `modelSpecBlocker(plan)` — no tick/phrase; blocks only when nothing to apply, sheet problems (unknown section, duplicate label, empty label) or unchecked rows. `modelSpecConfirmationPhrase` removed. `modelSpecsFromProduct` kept (tests) but no longer used by the page.
- **Server unchanged:** RPC `apply_model_common_specifications` (Owner only, exact product set re-checked, variant facts refused, add-only, corrections only for products still at the old approved value, stale refused) and the deferred future-product trigger; migration `202611200002` file **not edited, NOT applied**.
- **QA (VERIFIED):** `iphoneModelSpecs` + `specificationBulkUpload` + `securityRls` **42 / 42** — updated tests for the new blocker / sheet corrections / Status column / skipped rows / page wording (no tick, phrase, checkbox, copy-from-product; file read never calls the RPC; one RPC call after confirm), and a new **in-memory end-to-end test**: sheet with Verified + Unverified + TBD rows → only verified rows approved and added, existing different value kept, second sheet corrects Screen Size without a tick (all products at the old approved value follow), a future "Samsung Galaxy A37 PTA 8/256GB" receives the corrected list, "Samsung A37 Pro" gets nothing; existing Owner-only security, stale-set, ambiguous-title and future-product tests still pass. `tsc -b` PASS; ESLint 0 problems on changed files. Page rendered in an offline scratchpad harness (anon DEV read): sheet upload → preview with 3 skipped rows, Apply enabled, **no RPC request on file select**; Apply not clicked. Signed-in page not checked. Full suite not re-run.
- **Risks (§4U.7):** the sheet must name one exact model (the Owner picks the model in step 1; the sheet has no model column); Status words outside the list are treated as unverified; ChatGPT verification quality is outside the app — the app trusts the final sheet as approved by Owner rule.

### 4W. 2026-11-23/24 session — S26 FE stock-update fix, PDP product canvas, Pistachio thumbnail, typed-confirmation UX (VERIFIED unless marked)

**4W.1 Android Stock Update — S26 FE combined line (CLOSED; DEV + PROD LIVE)**
- **Symptom (Owner, PROD):** heading "Samsung S26 FE" not recognised; 6 rows "brand / phone name missing" (the real screen text is "Please check this line — the price or phone name is missing"; the "brand / phone name" wording does not exist in code); 0 matched.
- **Root cause (CONFIRMED by source inspection):** the Owner's list writes RAM/storage, a comma-separated colour list and the price on ONE line under a model-only heading (`Samsung S26 FE` / `8/256 GB - Pistachio, Graphite, Blueberry - Rs 235,000`). `expandSupplierBlocks` (`src/lib/supplierListBlocks.ts`) only accepted a capacity-only sub-heading, so the block ended at the `!capacity` branch; the heading stayed unparsed and each capacity line was parsed alone (no brand/model; the shared parser split the colours → 6 rows). Not a browser/cache issue. Earlier diagnosis also found that `decoration()` is not applied to single colour+price lines (bullets/emoji/invisible characters there still end a block) — documented, not changed.
- **Fix:** `readCapacityColoursPrice` + one rule in `expandSupplierBlocks`: under an active heading, a line that STARTS with RAM/storage (unit required without RAM), ENDS with a price, and has only plain colour labels between is rewritten in place to `Samsung S26 FE 8/256GB Pistachio, Graphite, Blueberry @ 235,000` (same line count); the shared parser (`catalogSheet.ts`, unchanged) expands the colours. ## 5. iPhone Stock Management (complete on DEV + PROD — do not redo)

> **2026-11-18:** the parser in `src/lib/iphoneStockUpdate.ts` gained the supplier block format + `HK Approved` note (PROD only, uncommitted, DEV pending) — see §4Q.

### 5.1 Rules (locked)
- New and Used are **separate products** in Mobile Phones: `Apple iPhone 15 Pro Max` (New) and `Apple iPhone 15 Pro Max (Used)` (`…-used`). Matched only by exact title and only if every variant has that condition; otherwise **held**.
- New variant/phone: opening stock **20**. Existing listed variant: price only, stock kept. Hidden listed variant: shown again with the list price. Missing variants: never touched. No quantity field.
- Condition and PTA only from the Owner's page choice or list words. Grade, battery health, cycles, warranty, RAM stay NULL. Karachi-only.
- `Physical Dual` = Dual Physical SIM. Owner-confirmed spellings (iPhone only): Blaxk → Black, Purppe → Purple, Blk → Black. A price line without a colour is Skipped.
- New phones are created as **drafts** (via `apply_catalog_bulk_import_v2`, action Create).
- Page `/admin/iphone-stock-update`: choose Condition + PTA → paste list → Check Changes → Update Website (DEV + PROD).

### 5.4 Warranty rules (current validator, DEV + PROD)
- **Used iPhones:** no warranty; NULL; publishing allowed. Exemption only for a real Apple product in Mobile Phones whose title ends with " (Used)" and whose every variant is Used. **Do not type a default warranty for Used iPhones.**
- **New / Box Pack iPhones:** warranty required — 1 year or 2 years, **only from Owner-confirmed product information**. **Owner rule changed 2026-11-18:** New iPhone warranty becomes **optional** (blank = NULL, no default; stated warranty kept) — migration `202611190001` **applied to DEV + PROD 2026-11-19**; Add Product change live on PROD Worker `584d37bb` (§4R).
- **Gadgets** (category `gadgets` itself): PTA, condition and warranty not required (§5A).
- **iPads, tablets and Apple MacBooks** (category `tablets`, or `laptops` with brand Apple): warranty optional for New and Used; PTA and condition must still be set (§5B).
- **Mobile Accessories** (category `mobile-accessories`, real data — migration `202611060001`, 2026-11-06): warranty **optional**; NULL allowed; Owner-confirmed warranties stay stored. PTA, condition, delivery, primary image and all other checks unchanged (§5E).
- **Android and all other products:** warranty rule unchanged.
- Other publishing checks unchanged: real classification, title, active brand/category, active variant, price > 0, delivery area, PTA and condition not unknown (except gadgets), primary image. A non-Apple product in `laptops` can never be published (`laptops_apple_brand_required`).

## 5B. Tablets & MacBooks (live DEV + PROD — do not redo)

- **Page** `/admin/tablet-stock-update` (nav "Tablets & MacBooks"): choose **Product Type** (iPad / Tablet / MacBook), **Condition** (New / Used) and, for iPad/Tablet, **Connectivity** (WiFi Only / Cellular — PTA Approved / Cellular — Non-PTA) → Paste List → Check Changes → preview (New Products / New Variants / Price Updates / Show Again / Needs Review) → Update Website (confirm names the website). Writes allowed DEV + PROD.
- **Category mapping (existing categories, no new ones):** iPad → `tablets` + Apple; other tablets → `tablets` (any brand except Apple); MacBook → `laptops` + Apple. **Windows laptops are never added.**
- **List formats:** one line `Galaxy Tab A8 4/64; 43,000` (RAM/storage, optional colour, price after `;`, `@`, or a comma-grouped amount); or a block — model heading, then a price line and a configuration line in either order (Owner-confirmed: `Macbook Air M5 Now Available` / `420500` / `16-512Gb Midnight` = MacBook Air M5, 16 GB, 512 GB, Midnight, Rs 420,500). "Now Available" is the only announcement phrase removed. `16-512Gb` = 16 GB RAM / 512 GB.
- **Smart Paste (`228553e`, live DEV + PROD — CLOSED):** natural supplier/WhatsApp lists are read as written: (a) model + RAM/storage heading without a price (`Macbook Air M5 16-256Gb`), then one **colour + price per line** (`SkyBlue 397,000`, `Silver @402,000`, `silver - 402,000`, `Midnight: 450,000`, `Rs …`; `Blue/Grey 210,000` = both colours one price); (b) one-line entries followed by **more storage lines of the same model with their own price** (`Samsung Tab S10 Lite 6-128Gb@98,500` / `6-256Gb @115,000`, noted "Model … read from line N above"); (c) config line under a model heading, then colour + price lines; `8GB-256GB` dash form; blank lines don't break a group. Needs Review (never guessed): colour without price; colour + price with no RAM/storage above; a configuration that already has a colour followed by colour lines; storage line without price; config with no model; brand/model/WiFi words never read as colours; duplicate colour lines; New/Used or PTA differing from the heading. Unknown colours (e.g. "Skyblue") still need the Owner's **Keep**. Compare/plan/write rules unchanged. Unit-less dash config (`8-128` without Gb) and typo units (`Gbb`) are not read — optional future work.
- **Nothing guessed — Needs Review:** unknown brand (e.g. Docomo), two prices or configurations in a row, a block whose price/config order changes, a configuration without a model, missing storage, unclear connectivity (or WiFi vs Cellular conflict), product type mismatch (Apple on Tablet, non-Apple on iPad/MacBook, laptop words on Tablet), duplicate titles/lines, product in another category, a website variant that differs only by a blank RAM/colour, other-condition variants, look-alike new products (Owner confirms "This is a new product"), unknown colour (Owner "Keep").
- **Rules:** New and Used separate products (Used title ends " (Used)"); exact-title matching only. Existing listed variant: price only, stock kept; hidden listed variant: shown again with the list price; missing variants/products untouched. New variant / new product: **draft**, stock **20**, **Karachi only**, warranty/grade/battery/cycles/SIM NULL, colour NULL when not written. PTA: WiFi → Not Applicable; Cellular → Owner-chosen Approved / Non-PTA; MacBook → Not Applicable. Variant identity = RAM + storage + colour + PTA + condition.
- **Server:** `apply_tablet_stock_product` (migration `202610270001`) — one product per call, all or nothing; scope `tablets` or Apple `laptops` only; MacBook PTA must be Not Applicable; SKUs `IP###` (tablets) / `MC###` (MacBooks); read-back inside the transaction. New products via `apply_catalog_bulk_import_v2` (Create, draft). iPhone RPC untouched.
- **Publishing:** warranty optional for tablets and Apple MacBooks (New + Used); PTA/condition/image still required; non-Apple laptops blocked.
- **Storefront:** `/collections/laptops-tablets` (same URL) is now **"Tablets & MacBooks"**: iPad, Android Tablets, **MacBooks** (`?type=macbooks` = `laptops` + Apple; old `?type=laptops` shows the same MacBooks; a non-Apple brand link shows nothing). Menu and drawer renamed; Acer/HP/Dell/Lenovo laptop links removed. `/collections/macbook` and homepage cards unchanged.
- **QA:** localhost (parser/plan 18 tests, RPC + validator on real migrations in PGlite 7 tests, collections 4 tests, Admin harness desktop/mobile); DEV migration + deploy verified; **Owner DEV real-write QA PASS** (MacBook Air M5, draft, MC001, stock 20, Rs 420,500 — Owner-reported). **Smart Paste:** tablet + RPC tests 33/33 PASS on a clean export of `228553e`; `build:dev` + `build:prod` PASS; DEV + PROD deployed and byte-verified 2026-10-28; **Owner PROD real-data QA PASS for MacBook and Tablets (Owner-reported).**

## 5C. Admin Catalog Bulk Publish (`704dc83`, live DEV + PROD — CLOSED, do not redo)

- **Where:** Admin → Catalog (`/admin/products`). Checkbox **only on Draft rows** (published/archived rows have none); **Select all drafts** (follows the current search; shows the count), **N selected**, **Clear selection**. Selection can mix categories.
- **Validate Selected:** re-reads the selected products fresh and runs the existing server validator `validate_product_for_publication` for each. Shows **Ready to Publish / Needs Review / Already Published / Skipped**, with the exact reason per product (plain words + code, e.g. "primary image required (primary_product_media_required)"). Never writes.
- **Publish Selected (n Ready):** disabled until a current validation exists; any tick/untick makes it stale. Confirmation shows environment + project ref, count and every product name; **PROD requires typing `PUBLISH <n>`**; double-click latch; leave-page warning while running.
- **Server re-validation:** reuses the existing Android bulk-publish engine unchanged (`src/lib/bulkPublish.ts` `runBulkPublish`, `src/lib/bulkPublishDb.ts`): per product it re-reads, re-validates, writes **only `publication_status = 'published'`**, then reads back (prices, variants, SKUs, media, stock unchanged). The `products_enforce_publication` trigger enforces the same validator, so a stale preview can never publish an invalid product. One failure never blocks others; failed products stay Draft. Results: Successfully Published / Failed–Needs Review / Already Published–Skipped with reasons; the list refreshes and the selection clears.
- **All category rules come from the server validator** (image, classification, variants, price, PTA/condition, warranty incl. used-iPhone exemption and optional tablet/MacBook warranty, gadget exemptions, delivery, Apple-only Laptops). No new validator, RPC or migration. Single-product "Validate & publish" in the editor is unchanged.
- **Files:** `src/lib/catalogBulkPublish.ts`, `src/admin/CatalogBulkPublish.tsx`, `src/admin/AdminCatalog.tsx`, `src/admin/admin.css` (Bulk Publish block at the end), `scripts/catalogBulkPublish.test.mjs`.
- **QA (Claude-verified):** localhost mocked-database harness — 12 browser checks PASS (mixed categories, stale preview, mid-run invalidation stays Draft, exactly-only `publication_status` writes, mobile 375 px); tests **28/28** (13 new + 15 engine) on a clean export of `704dc83`; typecheck, lint, `build:dev`, `build:prod` PASS; DEV + PROD guards PASS; live bundles byte-identical.
- **QA (Owner-reported):** DEV — 136 Drafts selected → 55 Ready, 81 Needs Review; a separate 3-product check → all 3 correctly blocked (missing Primary Image). PROD — 3 new gadgets selected → 0 Ready, 3 Needs Review (missing Primary Image), **Publish Selected (0) correctly disabled**. No product was published during QA.
- **Caution:** `npm run dev` / DEV hosting talk to the real DEV database — Publish Selected there really publishes DEV products. Validate Selected is read-only everywhere.
