**Why the database category stays `gadgets` (read-only analysis 2026-11-06):** the Admin product editor's Save always sends `publication_status: "draft"` (a category change there unpublishes); `products_enforce_publication` validates only when `publication_status` changes, so a direct category change would bypass validation; `mobile-accessories` publishing needs PTA, condition and warranty (all 22 variants have PTA/condition `unknown`; 12 products have no warranty); SKUs are immutable (`catalog_sku_immutable`) and the `mobile-accessories` prefix is `AC`, so GD SKUs would sit in Accessories.

**Files (preserve):** Shopify export `C:\Users\User\Downloads\products_export_1 (3).zip` (sha256 `904f21a8…`); import Excel `scratch\accessories-16-import\isolutions-catalog-accessories-16-gadgets.xlsx` (sha256 `1ef5110f…`); image reference `scratch\accessories-16-import\accessories-16-image-reference.xlsx` (sha256 `987406f1…`; 46 rows = 16 primary + 19 gallery + 11 excluded); approved images `scratch\accessories-16-import\approved-images\` (35 originals in 16 SKU folders, `01-PRIMARY…`). `scratch/` is excluded from Git (`.git/info/exclude`).

**Not done / known:** some original primaries are small (GD011 259×194, GD031 300×300, GD014 350×350); image ↔ colour links were not set (bulk workflow has no colour linking); no PDP description yet (§11). The other 19 products of the Owner's 35-product list (15 accessories blocked by PTA/condition/warranty for `mobile-accessories`, plus 4 with wrong/unverifiable images: Watch Ultra 2025, Amazfit Bip Max, Redmi Watch 6 Active / 6 Lite) were not imported (§11 #4).

**Update 2026-11-07:** real accessories are now published (§5E), so the Accessories category is in the public taxonomy and `/collections/mobile-accessories` lists the real accessories together with these 16 extras (existing `216e7dd` merge logic; no code change). The Owner's live screenshot shows new accessories on that page; Claude has not re-checked the page.

  - **Accessories content (OWNER-REPORTED):** 44 descriptions and 624 specs uploaded.
  - **5 remaining products:** files prepared in `E:\iSolutions-Phase2-Content\FINAL-UPLOAD-5\`; Owner upload not reported. Samsung Watch Fast Wireless Charger (Box) stays HOLD.
- **Manual Add / Edit Product** (`c38d02a`, `5a7155f`, `c9eda3a`; **PROD only**; `https://isolutionspakistan.com/admin/products/add`).
  - Every category is created as a Draft through `apply_catalog_bulk_import_v2`, with category rules locked.
  - **Live phones / tablets / MacBooks:** only columns the publish validator never reads are editable (description, SEO, placement, specs, stock, recommendations); prices and variants change through the Stock Update pages.
  - **Known limit:** live Gadget / Accessory variant edits are checked in the browser plus re-checked on the server after saving, not in one atomic transaction.
- **Bulk Image Upload by SKU** (`9e381a2` + fix `4549626`; **PROD only**; `https://isolutionspakistan.com/admin/media`, top section).
  - **Input:** files, a folder or a ZIP, named `<SKU>-<NN>.jpg/png/webp`.
  - **Preview:** matched products, Unmatched, Duplicates (SHA-256) and Errors (real format from the bytes, minimum 300 px).
  - **SKU range** is required on PRODUCTION.
  - **Writes:** image 01 becomes Primary only when the product has none; other images are appended. Three products upload in parallel, with each product's images re-read before writing and read back afterwards.
  - **Retry** checks recorded results against the database: saved images are skipped, and a Cloudinary upload whose row was not saved is saved without uploading again.
  - CSV report.
  - **Never** deletes, replaces, links colours or publishes. No migration / RPC / Edge Function change.

## 5H. Home Gadgets subcategories and storefront commerce features (2026-11-09/10 — CLOSED, PROD LIVE)

All PROD only (Owner-approved; DEV skipped). Localhost QA used a mocked-database harness; no real data was changed by Claude.

**5H.1 Home Gadgets 10 subcategories (`8fa72ad`, migration `202611090001`).**
- **Database:** `product_subcategories` (name, slug, sort order, active; parent must be `gadgets`; RLS: read only — public sees active rows, admins all; no write grants), 10 rows: Kitchen & Dining, Storage & Organizers, Home & Living, Bathroom Essentials, Health & Beauty, Gadgets & Accessories, Bags & Travel, Laundry & Cleaning, Sports & Outdoors, Kids & Toys. Nullable `products.subcategory_id` with a composite FK that keeps it in the product's own category.
- **RPCs:** `search_public_catalog` re-created with `p_subcategory_slugs` and a returned `subcategory`. New `admin_assign_product_subcategories(p_assignments jsonb)` — SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path=''`, requires `is_catalog_admin`, all-or-nothing validation, changes only `subcategory_id`; execute for `authenticated` only. The publish validator does not read the field.
- **Admin:** "Home Gadgets subcategory" dropdown + "Save subcategory" in the product editor; bulk page **`/admin/subcategory-upload`** ("Subcategories" in the nav): Excel/CSV (SKU, Subcategory, optional Product Name), preview (Assign / Change / Unchanged / errors), confirmation tick and on PROD the typed phrase `ASSIGN N SUBCATEGORIES`, read-back after saving.
- **Storefront:** the 10 subcategories after the existing 6 types in the Home Gadgets mega menu, mobile drawer and collection filter chips (`/collections/home-gadgets?type=<slug>`); the 16 Watches/Bands stay excluded; Drafts never appear. Live Home Gadgets = 199 products after the deploy.
- **Piyave assignment — CLOSED (OWNER-REPORTED, Owner-verified):** the Owner applied **178/178** from `E:\iSolutions-Piyave-Gadgets\Piyave-SKU-Subcategory-Assignment.xlsx` (sheets Assignments 178, Owner Review 14, Summary; `.csv` copy alongside) through the Admin bulk page. Do not repeat it. Claude prepared the file and the read-only preflight (178/178 OK) only.
- **14 products need an Owner subcategory decision** (sheet "Owner Review"): GD040, GD045, GD065, GD076, GD079, GD081, GD092, GD104, GD121, GD152, GD168, GD181, GD195, GD200. No assignment was guessed.

**5H.2 Home Gadgets mega menu — final design (`e34cbab`).** Same layout as the Mobile Accessories menu: 4 balanced columns, no section headings, promo image; links weight 400, colour `#808080`, orange `#F0643A` hover; labels wrap at 1080–1279 px so nothing overlaps. Other menus unchanged.

**5H.3 Add to Cart / Buy it Now on product cards (`a7d7fc1`).** Every collection and Shop grid, all categories. Desktop (mouse): buttons appear over the bottom of the image on hover/focus; touch / under 768 px: always shown under the price. Single variant → existing cart (stock limits kept), Buy it Now → `/checkout`; several variants → "Choose options", both buttons open the PDP (no variant is pre-selected); out of stock → disabled. Homepage cards keep their own buttons. Delivery rules stay enforced at checkout.

**5H.4 Sale badges + broken-image fix (`893f669`, rule changed by `d712c58`).** Orange "Sale · X%" badge top-left on collection / Shop cards (homepage badge style reused). A broken or missing product image now shows only the grey placeholder, so nothing overlaps the badge or the heart.

**5H.5 PDP compare-at alignment (`a206489`).** The crossed-out original price sits beside the selected variant's selling price, then "Save X%". The pricing logic was already correct (`validCompareAt()`; hidden when NULL, invalid or not higher); it updates when the variant changes.

**5H.6 Flash Sale & Deals checkbox (`d712c58`).**
- Admin product editor, Homepage Placement: the existing checkbox is now labelled **"Flash Sale & Deals"** and still saves `products.is_flash_sale` (no new field, no migration).
- **ON:** the published product appears in `/collections/deals`, gets the Sale badge, and is eligible for the homepage Flash Sale section (which already used this flag). **OFF:** not in Deals, no badge.
- Badge text: "Sale · X%" when the product has a valid compare-at price and one discount level across its discounted variants; otherwise "Sale".
- **Compare-at price is independent:** the crossed-out price and Save % still show on cards and PDP whenever a valid compare-at price exists, ticked or not.
- **PROD (read-only 2026-11-10): 0 products ticked, 0 variants with a compare-at price** → Deals and homepage Flash Sale are empty until the Owner configures them in Admin. No discount was created automatically.

## 7. Images (global rules)

**Owner-approved rules — every current and future category, New and Used, one shared `product_media` system:**
- **Official manufacturer image = Primary.** **Actual product photos (Owner) = Secondary.**
- Exact product/model/colour match. Official images need verified commercial usage rights.
- **Manual upload only** — no automatic discovery, scraping, replacement, deletion, reordering or publishing.
- Images stay saved when stock reaches zero, a variant is hidden or restocked.
- Existing 267 PROD Android images stay untouched (Unclassified).
- **Save original-resolution image files and upload them — never drag-and-drop Google Images thumbnails.**
- **iPhone image quality improvement is deferred: the Owner will replace low-resolution primaries later.**
- **Colour association:** an image's colour comes only from its stored `variantId`/`variantIds` (set in the Admin Media editor). An image without an association is a shared image.

**Code today:** Admin → Media (`/admin/media`) = Manual Product Image Upload (Missing Images list → Upload Images → choose Primary → environment-guarded upload). **Not implemented:** image type (Official/Actual), source, usage-rights fields, customer labels, official-primary publishing gate.

## 7A. Published product image-quality audit (PROD, read-only, 2026-11-02) — working list for the next session

**Scope and method (done — do not repeat):** all **103 published PROD products**, **293 images**. Original files were downloaded read-only (no Cloudinary derived assets created, no writes). Each image was graded on the product's own size inside the image (long side): **Poor / Blurry** < 400 px; **Needs Improvement** 400–679 px, or a third-party watermark, or the product filling < 35 % of the image, or heavy JPEG compression (estimated quality < 70); **Good** ≥ 680 px. The PDP main image shows the product at about 680 px.

**Results:**
- **22 products Poor / Blurry + 67 products Needs Improvement = 89 products** need image review / correction. 14 products are Good.
- **13 Poor primary images** and **9 watermarked images** (GSMArena, GSMFLOOR.PK, abenson.com) identified.
- **A product's grade is its weakest image.** A product in the list does not necessarily have a poor primary image — the weak image may be a secondary one.
- Limits: PDP-size sharpness was visually checked for 6 images only; watermarks were checked on candidate images.
- Detailed per-image output (`image-audit-results.csv` / `.md`, with media IDs and reasons) was produced in Claude's session scratchpad, **not in the repo**. The list below is the authoritative record.

**Status 2026-11-05:** the image correction work that used this analysis is **CLOSED** (§7C). This section is kept unchanged as the historical record — **do not repeat the analysis.** The source folder now lives at **`E:\product-images-bulk`** (122 files, outside the repository); the Git/`dist/` exposure risk below no longer applies.

**Status (as written 2026-11-04):** Phase A (read-only analysis) is **complete — do not repeat it**. The Owner put the upload **ON HOLD** on 2026-11-04. Nothing was uploaded, written, moved, renamed, committed or deployed. When work resumes, start from §7B.9 (pending decisions); the lists below are the authoritative record.

### 7B.1 Latest Owner decisions (2026-11-04)
- **AI-generated product images are allowed in principle** (this changes §7 "official = Primary" for this work only as far as the Owner decides; the role/label rule is still open — §7B.9).
- Upload **only images whose model and colour are verified correct**. Wrong-model / wrong-brand / out-of-scope images are excluded. Uncertain images stay **Needs Review**.
- **Appropriate AI disclosure is required** — how it is implemented is **undecided** (§7B.9 #3).
- Existing products only; no duplicate products, no new variants, no silent replacements, no automatic publishing. Localhost → DEV → PROD; every upload/write needs explicit Owner approval.
- **Bulk image upload is HOLD — not approved for execution.**

### 7B.2 Source folder (Owner-placed)
- Path: `E:\iSolutions Pakistan-Website\public\assets\product-images-bulk` — **122 files, 185,974,162 bytes (~186 MB), no subfolders**, all named `.png`/`.PNG`. All 122 open correctly (none corrupt).
- **Not ignored by Git, not tracked, not staged**; shows as one untracked entry `?? public/assets/product-images-bulk/` (so `git status --short` = **37** = the 36 original entries + this folder).
- **Folder still in place — not moved** (moving it is pending decision §7B.9 #1).

### 7B.3 Metadata findings
- **121 of 122 files are AI-generated:** each carries a signed C2PA manifest (`caBX` chunk) — action `c2pa.created`, softwareAgent **ChatGPT / gpt-image**, digitalSourceType `http://cv.iptc.org/newscodes/digitalsourcetype/trainedAlgorithmicMedia`, claim generator "OpenAI Media Service API", signer "OpenAI OpCo, LLC" (SSL.com / Trufo C2PA CAs), plus `c2pa.watermarked.unbound`. Sampled creation times are all **2026-11-02**.
- **`Samsung S23 ultra (Cream).png` is a JPEG (JFIF) with a .png extension**, 559×512, 153 KB, no C2PA — **source unknown, low resolution**. The upload validator (`validateSourceImage`, `src/lib/cloudinary.ts`) accepts it (checks magic bytes only).
- The signed upload transformation is `c_limit,w_3000,h_3000,q_90,fl_force_strip` (`supabase/functions/cloudinary-upload-signature/index.ts`) — **`fl_force_strip` removes the C2PA credentials from the stored Cloudinary master**, so AI provenance is lost after upload.
- Sizes: the AI files are ~1.5 MP (1254×1254, 1536×1024, 1122×1402, 1230×1278, …; long side ≥ 1254 px) — enough for the 680 px PDP size, but not "original-resolution official" files.
- **Exact duplicate (SHA-256 prefix `E11C0FDE708F`):** `Tecno Camon 50 (Black).png` = `TECNO Camon 50 pro (Black).png` (byte-identical).
- **Background defects:** checkerboard (fake transparency) baked in — `Samsung S26 plus (Black).png`, `Vivo Y05 (Platinum).png`; black background — `Samsung z fold 8 ultra (cream).png`.
- **Text drawn on the image:** spec claims — `Oppo A6 Blue.PNG` (8GB RAM 256GB ROM, 5100mAh, 45W, 50MP), `Realme 16 5G White.PNG` (6.57", 7000mAh 60W, Dual 50MP, IP69); carrier status bar "AT&T" — `Samsung S26 (Black).png`; model name on the screen — `Samsung A57 Lilac.PNG`, `Samsung A57 Navy.PNG`, `Honor X6c Black.PNG`, `Honor X5c+ Cyan.PNG`.
- **Wrong content (AI invented):** `Realme C100 Purple.PNG` shows "Redmi" branding; `Xiaomi Redmi 15 (Grey) (2).png` is a blue TECNO phone; `Oppo Reno 16 5G Volt.PNG` is a purple "Reno16 F 5G"; `Nothing Phone 1 Green.PNG` shows "CMF by Nothing".

### 7B.9 Upload workflow analysis, risks and PENDING decisions (none approved)
**Existing workflows (read-only review):**
- **Per-product Media tab** (`src/admin/MediaManager.tsx`, Admin → Catalog → product): **Upload image** (one file at a time; inserted as a **non-primary** gallery image at the end when the product already has images; alt text = filename), colour dropdown (`set_product_media_color_assignment`), **Make Primary** (`set_product_media_primary`; old primary stays as gallery), reorder, editable alt text. **Replace** and **Delete** call `cloudinary-delete-media` (destructive — must not be used). No dry run, no duplicate (hash) check, no typed PROD confirmation, no batch read-back.
- **Bulk Product Images** (`/admin/media`, `src/admin/BulkProductImages.tsx`, `src/lib/bulkImageUpload.ts`, `bulkImagePreflight.ts`): preflight, exact DEV/PROD target check, typed `PRODUCTION`, one product at a time, media re-read before writing ("Media changed since review"), never deletes, "Add images" / "Add and make primary" (old primary kept), read-back verification, orphan reporting. **Limitations:** in the current UI local files can only be attached to products in its **Missing Images** list (products without a primary) — **all 89 products already have a primary, so this page cannot target them without a code change**; no colour linking; filename-based matching; no hash/resolution/AI-origin check; max 10 files per manual batch.
- Direct script / API upload: not allowed (bypasses Admin guards, needs privileged credentials).
- **AI disclosure:** no image-type / AI / source field and no customer-facing label exists. The only existing place is the editable **alt text** (stored, not visible on the page). Visible disclosure = new development (needs separate approval).

**Source-folder exposure — BLOCKING RISK until resolved:** the folder is not ignored; `git add -A`/`git add .` would stage ~186 MB; **Vite copies `public/` into `dist/`** on every build and Firebase deploys `dist/` (ignores only dotfiles, `node_modules`, `firebase.json`), so a working-tree build + deploy would publish the files at `/assets/product-images-bulk/…`. Today it is protected only by process (clean `git archive` exports exclude untracked files; commits via a temporary index). `npm run dev` serves them on localhost only.

**Proposed execution approach (NOT approved):** move the folder outside the repo → localhost/DEV rehearsal on 1 product (e.g. Oppo A6c) via the per-product Media tab → Claude read-only DEV check → PROD in batches of ~10 products, Owner doing the clicks (Upload image once per file, set colour, optional Make Primary; never Replace/Delete) → after each batch a Claude read-only PROD read-back (image count +n, no old image lost, one primary per product, colour links correct, no duplicates). Nothing published automatically.

**Pending Owner decisions (not approved):**
1. Move the image folder outside the repository (recommended: `E:\iSolutions Pakistan-Image-Source\product-images-bulk\`, byte-verified; alternative `.git/info/exclude` protects Git only, not builds).
2. Gallery-only versus making selected uploaded images Primary (Owner names product + colour; never guessed).
3. Customer-visible AI disclosure (new development) versus the existing alt-text limitation (stored, not visible).
4. Final upload method (per-product Media tab as-is, or approved development to let Bulk Product Images target products that already have a primary and/or link colours).
5. DEV rehearsal approval, then PROD upload approval per batch.

- **Owner image-quality rule (2026-11-04):** an image is defective only if it is genuinely blurry, distorted, watermarked or the wrong model/colour — **low resolution alone is not a defect**; clear Primary/Gallery images stay unchanged; uncertain cases stay Pending.
- **Focused read-only visual review (PROD, 2026-11-04):** the 40 products of §7B.5 that had verified candidates, 141 images (40 Primary + 101 Gallery). Result: **7 confirmed defects** and **12 pending cases**:

  - Pending (12): A07 Primary + Gallery and A17 Gallery (edge artifact, no exact file); Fold 5, S24 Ultra, S26 Plus Gallery (colour not sold); V70 FE Primary and X300 FE ×2 Gallery (colour unclear); City 200 Gallery (possible watermark); Note 60x and Poco C85 Primary (softness uncertain).

## 7D. Admin Media UI fixes (`f8b19e1`, `24de477`, live DEV + PROD — CLOSED)

- **Hidden action buttons (`f8b19e1`):** the storefront's global `img { height:100% }` stretched each Media card image to the whole card, pushing Make primary / Replace / Delete outside the card under the next row. Fix: `height:auto` on `.media-grid article > img`. Verified on live PROD (Samsung S23 Ultra, 5 cards, desktop + mobile).
- **Primary card labels (`f8b19e1`):** the storefront `.primary` class turned the primary card's labels white → `color:var(--a-ink)`.
- **Primary card orange hover (`24de477`):** the storefront `.primary:hover { background:#F0643A }` beat `.media-grid article` → `background:#fff` on `.media-grid article.primary`. Orange border and PRIMARY badge kept. Verified live DEV + PROD, desktop + mobile.
- Media upload, colour association, Make Primary, Replace and Delete behaviour unchanged.

## 7E. Admin Media Multiple Image Upload (`6bf7aee`, live DEV + PROD, 2026-11-08)

- **What:** the per-product Media tab button is now **"Upload images"** (multi-select). One file → the unchanged single-upload path. Several files → a batch list with per-image progress bar and status (Queued / Uploading % / Uploaded / Failed / Skipped) and a summary.
- **Rules:** images upload **one at a time** through the same signed `cloudinary-upload-signature` → Cloudinary → `product_media` insert as the single upload (same `variant_id: null`, binary-metadata guard, `sort_order` = current count, `is_primary` only when the product has no images). The batch is **add-only**: it clears a pending Replace click and never replaces or deletes. Duplicates are skipped before upload: same content twice in one selection (SHA-256), already uploaded to this product in this browser session, or a file name equal to an existing image's alt text. On partial failure successful uploads stay; **"Retry failed (n)"** re-sends only failed items. Replace (single input), Delete, reorder, Make primary and colour association are unchanged.
- **Not changed:** no migration, RPC, Edge Function, storage, Cloudinary configuration or permission change.
- **Duplicate-guard limit:** there is no stored source-file hash, so an identical image uploaded in an earlier session under a different file name is not detected.
- **QA (Claude-verified):** focused tests 41/41 (new `productMediaBatch` 7 + existing media upload / PDP media sync / bulk image upload), unchanged `productMediaUpload` source assertions PASS; `tsc -b`, eslint, `build:dev` PASS; clean-export focused tests 14/14; mocked-Supabase localhost harness (no DEV/PROD connection): multi-select, duplicate skip, partial failure, retry-only-failed, re-select skip, single Primary, Replace inherits Primary + deletes old, Make primary, colour association, cancelled Replace does not overwrite. DEV + PROD live bundles byte-identical (§4).
- **Verification limit:** the authenticated DEV/PROD Admin Media screen was **not** opened by Claude (no Owner session; Claude never enters passwords). The Owner's 2026-11-08 PROD upload of the 41 images is OWNER-REPORTED (§5E.11).

## 8. Specifications

- **Storage:** `product_specifications` (section, label, value, sort_order; unique per product + section + label). Customers see specs only on published products.
- **Spec Upload (`/admin/spec-upload`, all categories):** Download Template → fill → upload (checked, nothing saved) → **Save Specifications**. Columns: Product Name · Product Slug or Product ID · Section · Label · Value. Exact matching only. Per-product all-or-nothing save via `apply_product_specifications`. PROD needs the typed phrase `APPLY <n> SPECS`.
- **Stock Updates never create specifications.** Tablet/MacBook specs only from exact verified official sources with Owner approval, through Spec Upload.
- **PROD status:** 943 Android + 105 Used iPhone = **1,048** (verified 2026-10-24) **+ 25 tablet/MacBook specs** (Apple MacBook Air M5 8, Samsung A11 7, Samsung Tab S10 Lite 10 — saved by the Owner via Spec Upload, Claude read-only verified 2026-10-30). **Tablet/MacBook specifications CLOSED** — no repeat research or upload.
- No specification task is pending. New products get specs only from exact verified official sources with Owner approval.

## 9. PDP (product page)

### 9.0 iPhone Product Information + WhatsApp Ask Seller (`4c17c0b`, live DEV + PROD)
Scope: iPhones only (`apple` + `mobile-phones`). Block from the selected variant's stored data: Condition, PTA Status (not_applicable hidden), SIM Type, Battery Health (hidden on New), Warranty (hidden when NULL). Missing facts → field-specific WhatsApp **Ask Seller** (`923132344912`), no customer data. iPhones show warranty only in the block.

### 9.1 iPhone 13 (Used) image sizing (`9176c8d`, closed)
Slug-scoped Cloudinary `e_trim:10` on main image + thumbnails with fallback to the original. Owner verified. The "pink" image has no colour association (shared image) — Owner data fix in Admin Media.

### 9.2 PDP colour ↔ image alignment (`626f0f0`, live DEV + PROD)
Page load selects the primary image's in-stock variant; colour select shows its own image; thumbnail/arrow selects the image's colour **only if in stock in the selected storage** — image browsing never switches storage, variant or price.

### 9.3 Gadgets PDP V3 (`86af7c4`, live DEV + PROD)
See §5A. Gadgets only; uses `src/lib/pdpDescription.ts` (`descriptionParagraphs`, `gadgetWhatsappOrderUrl`, `gadgetDeliveryInfo`).

### 9.4 Premium Mobile PDP (`e63e639`, live DEV + PROD — CLOSED, do not redo)
- **Scope:** category `mobile-phones` only (Android, New iPhone, Used iPhone). `PDP` in `src/StorefrontApp.tsx` sets `isPhonePdp` and adds the class `pdp-page--phone`; every new rule in `src/styles.css` is scoped to it (the floating WhatsApp rules use `body:has(.pdp-page--phone)`). **Gadgets PDP V3 and Tablets & MacBooks PDPs are unchanged** (markup order and styling).
- **Done:** premium gallery (white image card, thumbnails, arrows); purchase panel card (brand, title, navy price, "Save %" pill, stock pill); storage/colour selectors (selected = navy, colour names wrap to 2 lines); iPhone Product Information block restyled (label above value; Ask Seller pill); Add to Cart / Buy Now (equal height, hover/active/disabled/focus states); unified trust card (same wording; single column on mobile); light specification cards (balanced columns — exactly 4 groups sit 2 × 2; narrower label column); Frequently Bought Together light cards; mobile description heading 26 px with tighter spacing.
- **Keyboard order:** on phones Frequently Bought Together is rendered **after** the purchase buttons in the markup (`frequentlyBoughtTogether` const), so visual and Tab order match. Gadgets still render it before the buttons.
- **Discount badge:** `validCompareAt` only returns a compare-at price that exists and is higher than the price; on phones the badge is also hidden when it rounds to 0 % (the struck compare-at price still shows). No fabricated discounts.
- **Mobile WhatsApp:** at ≤600 px on phone PDPs the floating chat button becomes a 30 × 48 px tab docked in the right gutter (same link and label) — 0 overlaps measured at 360/375/390/412/430 px. Other pages keep the round button.
- **Unchanged:** prices, stock, variants, storage/colour selection, colour ↔ image alignment (§9.2), image sizing/fallback, cart, Buy Now/checkout, warranty/PTA/condition/SIM/battery logic, specification data, WhatsApp links, URLs, Supabase queries/RPCs/schema.
- **QA:** localhost harness with a mocked Supabase client (no DEV/PROD connection, no env files; files lived only in the session scratchpad, not in the repo) — desktop/tablet/mobile visual checks, Tab order, discount cases, overlap scan; real Android phone review over LAN (Owner-reported PASS); targeted tests 127/127, typecheck, `build:dev`, `build:prod` PASS; DEV + PROD deploy byte-verified (§4).

### 9.5 Mobile footer, YouTube icon, Feedback-4 (`5b32024`, live DEV + PROD — CLOSED, do not redo)
Storefront-wide (not a PDP change), recorded here with the other storefront UI work.
- **Mobile footer (`src/styles.css`, only inside `@media (max-width:600px)`):** Bank Transfer / Cash on Delivery cards fixed 52 px high with 40 px artwork (a percentage image height did not resolve in the auto-sized grid row, so the 120×44 artwork overflowed the card); cards stretch to the content edges; Policies column spans the full row so its longest link stays on one line; footer bottom padding `84px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` keeps the copyright / "Powered by GrowthifyEdge" lines clear of the floating WhatsApp button; `text-wrap:balance` on the copyright. Floating WhatsApp button `right: max(16px, 12px + env(safe-area-inset-right))`. The phone-PDP WhatsApp edge tab (§9.4) is unchanged. Desktop unchanged.
- **YouTube icon (`src/StorefrontApp.tsx` Footer):** the rounded rectangle and the play triangle were two paths filled with the same colour, so the triangle was invisible. Now one path with `fillRule="evenodd"` — the triangle is cut out and shows the button background (white; light orange on hover). Link `https://www.youtube.com/@iSolutionOfficial` and label "YouTube" unchanged; other icons unchanged.
- **Feedback-4 (`public/assets/feedback/Feedback-4.png`, homepage reviews, referenced in `StorefrontApp.tsx`):** served from the repo (not Cloudinary). Replaced by the Owner's corrected image (official iSolutions Pakistan logo on the bag, box and Thank You card), 1122×1402, unchanged by Claude. Other feedback images untouched. Firebase uses its default 1-hour browser cache for it (no custom headers).
- **QA:** localhost 320/360/375/390/430/1440 px (equal 52 px cards, no horizontal overflow, WhatsApp fully in view, Feedback-4 same frame as Feedback-3); Owner real Android QA over LAN PASS (Owner-reported); targeted tests 127/127, typecheck, `build:dev` PASS; DEV + PROD live file checks (§4).

- **27 optimized WebP assets** in `public/assets` (original PNGs kept): 14 homepage banners (desktop + mobile), header/footer logo `isolutions-pakistan-logo-v2-256.webp`, 12 Shop By Category `-400.webp` images. Favicon still uses the original PNG logo.
- **Reliable carousel (`HomeHeroCarousel`):** only the first banner is requested up front (high priority); neighbours prefetch after it loads; one desktop/mobile variant per device; the visible banner switches only after the target has loaded and decoded; failed banners keep the current one and are skipped by the 4-second auto-rotation.
- Tests `scripts/homepagePerformance.test.mjs` (CRLF-agnostic since `31f24ca`). **Owner confirmed website performance and visuals satisfactory (Owner-reported).**

### 9.9 Samsung S26 PDP/cart variant display (`2b64fa0`, live DEV + PROD — CLOSED) + S26 PROD data (CLOSED)
- **Investigation (read-only, 2026-11-04):** the wrong variant was never added — the cart stores the selected `variantId` and checkout prices server-side. The issue was display only: shared (unlinked) images showed every colour, a single-colour storage showed no colour name, and the cart showed the browsed gallery image with no colour/storage.
- **Code (`2b64fa0`):** PDP names the colour even when the selected storage has one colour, if a storage choice exists (e.g. 12GB/256GB → Black); single-variant products (gadgets) unchanged. Cart stores a display-only `variantLabel` (e.g. "12GB/256GB · Black") and the selected variant's own image (`mediaForVariant`), shown under the title. `variantId`, price and checkout payload unchanged; carts saved earlier show no label.
- **PROD data (Owner-approved, via Admin Studio):** colour associations — Black image → MB037 + MB040 (hidden); White images → MB041. **Volt → Violet** on MB042 (12/512, active) and MB038 (12/256, hidden) — only `color_finish` changed (before/after snapshot: price, stock, IDs and all other fields identical). The Owner separately replaced S26 media (Black image is now Primary; a new image linked to the Violet variants).
- **Live PROD (2026-11-05):** 12GB/256GB → Black, Rs 289,000; 12GB/512GB → White / Violet, Rs 325,000; browsing images never changes the variant; no "Volt" on the page.

### 9.10 Gadgets PDP mobile WhatsApp overlap (`43101a6`, live DEV + PROD — CLOSED)
- **Root cause:** the phone PDP's mobile override docks the floating WhatsApp chat as a slim edge tab; gadget PDPs had no such override, so the 50 px round button (bottom-right) covered Quantity, Add to Cart, Buy Now and Order on WhatsApp.
- **Fix (≤600 px, gadget PDP only):** `pdp-page--gadget` class; the same slim 30×48 tab as the phone PDP; `.pdp-purchase` right padding 32 px so Share/Wishlist and all buying controls stay clear. Desktop and phone PDP unchanged.
- **Verified:** localhost 320/360/375/390/414/430, DEV 375, PROD 320/375/430 — overlap 0, no horizontal scroll, WhatsApp tab clickable.

### 9.11 Homepage Bundle Offers alignment (`43101a6`, live DEV + PROD — CLOSED)
- **Root cause:** on the two-column layout the promo banner started level with the section heading (not the cards) and its aspect-ratio height differed from the cards grid, so tops and bottoms did not line up.
- **Fix (≥821 px only):** heading row on top; cards grid and banner share one row; the banner keeps its full image and aspect ratio (1.10) and the cards stretch to its height. Mobile (≤820 px) stacked layout unchanged; images unchanged.
- **Verified:** localhost 821/900/1100/1440/1920 aligned and 320/375/820 unchanged; DEV 1440 + 375; PROD 1024 + 1440 aligned, 375 unchanged.

## 10. Bulk Import — current design (`7968015`, live DEV + PROD)

`/admin/bulk-import` = **Excel Create-only workflow**: Prepare Import Workbook (product types Mobile Phone, Tablet, Laptop, Accessory, Gadget), Raw stock lines → Generate Excel, Download Blank Template, Upload Excel, preview. Every row is `Create`; new products are **Drafts**; existing products → NEEDS REVIEW, never updated/replaced/hidden. Environment banner; **PROD typed phrase `CREATE <n> PRODUCTS`**. Legacy tools removed from the Admin UI (files remain, unmounted). `apply_catalog_bulk_import_v2` is shared by Android/iPhone/Gadgets/Tablets Stock Updates.
**Note:** Bulk Import code was not changed for Tablets & MacBooks, so it can still create a non-Apple Laptop **draft**; the validator blocks publishing it.

## 12. Decision record — Stock Update per category (done)

The Owner chose **per-category Stock Update pages** in Admin Studio so the website can be updated without Claude: Android, iPhone (New + Used), Gadgets, and Tablets & MacBooks. Each creates new products as drafts through the shared `apply_catalog_bulk_import_v2` and updates existing products through its own all-or-nothing RPC. Bulk Import stays as the Excel Create-only path (e.g. accessories).

## 14. How to start a fresh Claude Code chat
